============================================================
SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
============================================================

This file is rebuilt from the current Cinerific workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.

The snapshot intentionally ignores:
- .git
- .gradle
- .idea
- app/build
- build
- .kotlin
- .tmp
- .cache
- .DS_Store
- local.properties
- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
- *.jks
- *.keystore

Binary image and screenshot files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.

     1	package com.prahlin.cinerific
     2	
     3	import android.app.UiModeManager
     4	import android.content.pm.ActivityInfo
     5	import android.graphics.Color
     6	import android.os.Build
     7	import android.os.Bundle
     8	import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
     9	import androidx.activity.SystemBarStyle
    10	import androidx.activity.compose.setContent
    11	import androidx.activity.enableEdgeToEdge
    12	import androidx.core.view.WindowCompat
    13	import com.prahlin.cinerific.ui.CinerificApp
    14	import com.prahlin.cinerific.ui.theme.CinerificTheme
    15	
    16	class MainActivity : ComponentActivity() {
    17	    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
    18	        requestedOrientation = ActivityInfo.SCREEN_ORIENTATION_FULL_SENSOR
    19	        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
    20	            getSystemService(UiModeManager::class.java).setApplicationNightMode(
    21	                UiModeManager.MODE_NIGHT_YES
    22	            )
    23	        }
    24	        enableEdgeToEdge(
    25	            statusBarStyle = SystemBarStyle.dark(Color.TRANSPARENT),
    26	            navigationBarStyle = SystemBarStyle.dark(Color.BLACK),
    27	        )
    28	        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
    29	        window.statusBarColor = Color.TRANSPARENT
    30	        window.navigationBarColor = Color.BLACK
    31	        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.P) {
    32	            window.navigationBarDividerColor = Color.BLACK
    33	        }
    34	        WindowCompat.getInsetsController(window, window.decorView).apply {
    35	            isAppearanceLightStatusBars = false
    36	            isAppearanceLightNavigationBars = false
    37	        }
    38	        setContent {
    39	            CinerificTheme {
    40	                CinerificApp()
    41	            }
    42	        }
    43	    }
    44	}

   150	
   151	@Composable
   152	fun CinerificApp() {
   153	    var showHome by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }
   154	    var signedInProfile by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(CinerificProfile.Guest) }
   155	    var signInSessionId by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(0) }
   156	    var selectedLanguage by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(CinerificLanguage.English) }
   157	    var introSnapshot by rememberSaveable(stateSaver = CinerificIntroSnapshotSaver) {
   158	        mutableStateOf(CinerificIntroSnapshot())
   159	    }
   160	    var introView by remember { mutableStateOf<CinerificIntroView?>(null) }
   161	    var welcomeInputBlockedUntilMillis by remember { mutableStateOf(0L) }
   162	    var signOutLoadingVisible by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
   163	
   164	    fun selectProfile(profile: CinerificProfile) {
   165	        if (SystemClock.uptimeMillis() < welcomeInputBlockedUntilMillis) return
   166	        introSnapshot = CinerificIntroSnapshot()
   167	        signedInProfile = profile
   168	        signInSessionId += 1
   169	        signOutLoadingVisible = false
   170	        showHome = true
   171	    }
   172	
   173	    BackHandler(enabled = !showHome && introSnapshot.activeFlowName.isNotBlank()) {
   174	        introView?.handleSystemBack()
   175	    }
   176	
   177	    Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
   178	        AndroidView(
   179	            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
   180	            factory = { context ->
   181	                CinerificIntroView(context).apply {
   182	                    introView = this
   183	                    onIntroSnapshotChanged = { snapshot ->
   184	                        if (introSnapshot != snapshot) {
   185	                            introSnapshot = snapshot
   186	                        }
   187	                    }
   188	                    restoreIntroSnapshot(introSnapshot)
   189	                    onAvatarSelected = ::selectProfile
   190	                }
   191	            },
   192	            update = { view ->
   193	                view.onIntroSnapshotChanged = { snapshot ->
   194	                    if (introSnapshot != snapshot) {
   195	                        introSnapshot = snapshot
   196	                    }
   197	                }
   198	                view.restoreIntroSnapshot(introSnapshot)
   199	                view.onAvatarSelected = ::selectProfile
   200	            }
   201	        )
   202	
   203	        if (showHome) {
   204	            CinerificLocalizedResources(selectedLanguage) {
   205	                CinerificMainExperience(
   206	                    signInSessionId = signInSessionId,
   207	                    signedInProfile = signedInProfile,
   208	                    selectedLanguage = selectedLanguage,
   209	                    onLanguageSelected = { selectedLanguage = it },
   210	                    onSignOutLoadingStarted = { signOutLoadingVisible = true },
   211	                    onSignOut = {
   212	                        signOutLoadingVisible = true
   213	                        welcomeInputBlockedUntilMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() + 500L
   214	                        signedInProfile = CinerificProfile.Guest
   215	                        introSnapshot = CinerificIntroSnapshot()
   216	                        showHome = false
   217	                        signOutLoadingVisible = false
   218	                    }
   219	                )
   220	            }
   221	        }
   222	
   223	        if (signOutLoadingVisible) {
   224	            SignOutLoadingOverlay(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize())
   225	        }
   226	    }
   227	}
   228	
   229	internal enum class CinerificDestination {
   230	    Home,
   231	    Movies,
   232	    Shows,
   233	    Favorites,
   234	    Settings,
   235	    ProgramDetails
   236	}
   237	
   238	internal enum class CinerificProfile(
   239	    @DrawableRes val avatarResId: Int,
   240	    @DrawableRes val nameResId: Int
   241	) {
   242	    Steve(R.drawable.steve_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.steve_name),
   243	    Martin(R.drawable.martin_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.martin_name),
   244	    Janny(R.drawable.janny_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.janny_name),
   245	    Guest(R.drawable.guest_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.guest_name)
   246	}
   247	
   248	private data class CinerificDeviceIdentity(
   249	    val manufacturer: String,
   250	    val modelName: String,
   251	    val hardwareModel: String,
   252	    val releaseYear: Int?
   253	) {
   254	    val stableId: String
   255	        get() = Base64.encodeToString(
   256	            "$manufacturer|$hardwareModel".lowercase().toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8),
   257	            Base64.URL_SAFE or Base64.NO_WRAP or Base64.NO_PADDING
   258	        )
   259	
   260	    val displayName: String
   261	        get() = buildString {
   262	            append(manufacturer)
   263	            append(' ')
   264	            append(modelName)
   265	            releaseYear?.let { year ->
   266	                append(" (")
   267	                append(year)
   268	                append(')')
   269	            }
   270	        }
   271	}
   272	
   273	private fun currentCinerificDevice(): CinerificDeviceIdentity {
   274	    val rawManufacturer = Build.MANUFACTURER.trim().ifBlank { "Unknown" }
   275	    val manufacturer = rawManufacturer.lowercase().replaceFirstChar { character ->
   276	        character.titlecase()
   277	    }
   278	    val hardwareModel = Build.MODEL.trim().ifBlank { "Unknown model" }
   279	    val normalizedManufacturer = rawManufacturer.lowercase()
   280	    val normalizedModel = hardwareModel.uppercase()
   281	    val knownModel = when {
   282	        normalizedManufacturer == "samsung" && normalizedModel.startsWith("SM-G990") ->
   283	            "Galaxy S21 FE 5G" to 2022
   284	        else -> hardwareModel to null
   285	    }
   286	    return CinerificDeviceIdentity(
   287	        manufacturer = manufacturer,
   288	        modelName = knownModel.first,
   289	        hardwareModel = hardwareModel,
   290	        releaseYear = knownModel.second
   291	    )
   292	}
   293	
   294	private fun accountDevicePrefix(profile: CinerificProfile): String =
   295	    "account:${profile.name}"
   296	
   297	private fun recordAccountDeviceUse(
   298	    preferences: SharedPreferences,
   299	    profile: CinerificProfile,
   300	    device: CinerificDeviceIdentity
   301	) {
   302	    val accountPrefix = accountDevicePrefix(profile)
   303	    val idsKey = accountPrefix + ACCOUNT_DEVICE_IDS_KEY_SUFFIX
   304	    val devicePrefix = "$accountPrefix:device:${device.stableId}"
   305	    val deviceIds = preferences.getStringSet(idsKey, emptySet()).orEmpty().toMutableSet()
   306	    deviceIds += device.stableId
   307	    preferences.edit()
   308	        .putStringSet(idsKey, deviceIds)
   309	        .putString("$devicePrefix:manufacturer", device.manufacturer)
   310	        .putString("$devicePrefix:model", device.modelName)
   311	        .putInt("$devicePrefix:release_year", device.releaseYear ?: 0)
   312	        .putInt(
   313	            "$devicePrefix:use_count",
   314	            preferences.getInt("$devicePrefix:use_count", 0) + 1
   315	        )
   316	        .putLong("$devicePrefix:last_used", System.currentTimeMillis())
   317	        .apply()
   318	}
   319	
   320	private fun mostUsedDeviceName(
   321	    preferences: SharedPreferences,
   322	    profile: CinerificProfile
   323	): String? {
   324	    val accountPrefix = accountDevicePrefix(profile)
   325	    val deviceIds = preferences
   326	        .getStringSet(accountPrefix + ACCOUNT_DEVICE_IDS_KEY_SUFFIX, emptySet())
   327	        .orEmpty()
   328	    val mostUsedDeviceId = deviceIds.maxWithOrNull(
   329	        compareBy<String> { deviceId ->
   330	            preferences.getInt("$accountPrefix:device:$deviceId:use_count", 0)
   331	        }.thenBy { deviceId ->
   332	            preferences.getLong("$accountPrefix:device:$deviceId:last_used", 0L)
   333	        }
   334	    ) ?: return null
   335	    val devicePrefix = "$accountPrefix:device:$mostUsedDeviceId"
   336	    val manufacturer = preferences.getString("$devicePrefix:manufacturer", null) ?: return null
   337	    val modelName = preferences.getString("$devicePrefix:model", null) ?: return null
   338	    val releaseYear = preferences.getInt("$devicePrefix:release_year", 0)
   339	    return buildString {
   340	        append(manufacturer)
   341	        append(' ')
   342	        append(modelName)
   343	        if (releaseYear > 0) {
   344	            append(" (")
   345	            append(releaseYear)
   346	            append(')')
   347	        }
   348	    }
   349	}
   350	
   351	@Composable
   352	private fun CinerificMainExperience(
   353	    signInSessionId: Int,
   354	    signedInProfile: CinerificProfile,
   355	    selectedLanguage: CinerificLanguage,
   356	    onLanguageSelected: (CinerificLanguage) -> Unit,
   357	    onSignOutLoadingStarted: () -> Unit,
   358	    onSignOut: () -> Unit
   359	) {
   360	    var destination by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf(CinerificDestination.Home) }
   361	    var detailOriginDestination by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) {
   362	        mutableStateOf(CinerificDestination.Home)
   363	    }
   364	    var selectedProgramTitle by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf("Sink or Swim") }
   365	    var favoriteProgramTitles by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(emptyList<String>()) }
   366	    var userProgramRatings by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(emptyMap<String, Int>()) }
   367	    val context = LocalContext.current
   368	    val playbackStatsPreferences = remember(context) {
   369	        context.getSharedPreferences(PLAYBACK_STATS_PREFERENCES, 0)
   370	    }
   371	    val accountDevicePreferences = remember(context) {
   372	        context.getSharedPreferences(ACCOUNT_DEVICE_PREFERENCES, 0)
   373	    }
   374	    var mostUsedDeviceName by remember(signedInProfile) {
   375	        mutableStateOf(mostUsedDeviceName(accountDevicePreferences, signedInProfile))
   376	    }
   377	    var programPlayCounts by remember {
   378	        mutableStateOf(
   379	            playbackStatsPreferences.all.mapNotNull { (key, value) ->
   380	                if (!key.startsWith(PLAYBACK_COUNT_KEY_PREFIX)) return@mapNotNull null
   381	                val count = value as? Int ?: return@mapNotNull null
   382	                key.removePrefix(PLAYBACK_COUNT_KEY_PREFIX) to count
   383	            }.toMap()
   384	        )
   385	    }
   386	    var programPlaytimeMillis by remember {
   387	        mutableStateOf(
   388	            playbackStatsPreferences.all.mapNotNull { (key, value) ->
   389	                if (!key.startsWith(PLAYBACK_TIME_KEY_PREFIX)) return@mapNotNull null
   390	                val durationMillis = value as? Long ?: return@mapNotNull null
   391	                key.removePrefix(PLAYBACK_TIME_KEY_PREFIX) to durationMillis
   392	            }.toMap()
   393	        )
   394	    }
   395	    var mostRecentlyPlayedTitle by remember {
   396	        mutableStateOf(
   397	            playbackStatsPreferences.getString(MOST_RECENTLY_PLAYED_TITLE_KEY, null)
   398	        )
   399	    }
   400	    var catalogRouteDestinationName by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf("") }
   401	    var catalogRouteGenreName by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf("") }
   402	    var catalogRouteModeName by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf("") }
   403	    var favoritesFullPromptRequestId by remember { mutableStateOf(0) }
   404	    var autoLogoutEnabled by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }
   405	    var userInitiatedPlaybackActive by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
   406	    var activePlaybackTitle by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
   407	    var activePlaybackLastTickMillis by remember { mutableStateOf(0L) }
   408	    var lastInteractionMillis by remember { mutableStateOf(SystemClock.uptimeMillis()) }
   409	    var primaryDestinationCanScroll by remember(destination) { mutableStateOf<Boolean?>(null) }
   410	    var portraitBottomNavHiddenFraction by remember(signInSessionId) { mutableFloatStateOf(1f) }
   411	    var portraitBottomNavHeightPx by remember { mutableFloatStateOf(1f) }
   412	    var showSignOutConfirmation by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf(false) }
   413	    var signOutInProgress by remember(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf(false) }
   414	    val destinationStateHolder = rememberSaveableStateHolder()
   415	    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
   416	    val configuration = LocalConfiguration.current
   417	    val isPortrait = configuration.screenHeightDp > configuration.screenWidthDp
   418	    val usesScrollLinkedPortraitBottomNav = destination in setOf(
   419	        CinerificDestination.Home,
   420	        CinerificDestination.Movies,
   421	        CinerificDestination.Shows,
   422	        CinerificDestination.Favorites,
   423	        CinerificDestination.Settings
   424	    )
   425	
   426	    LaunchedEffect(signInSessionId, signedInProfile) {
   427	        recordAccountDeviceUse(
   428	            preferences = accountDevicePreferences,
   429	            profile = signedInProfile,
   430	            device = currentCinerificDevice()
   431	        )
   432	        mostUsedDeviceName = mostUsedDeviceName(accountDevicePreferences, signedInProfile)
   433	    }
   434	    val portraitBottomNavScrollConnection = remember(
   435	        isPortrait,
   436	        destination,
   437	        primaryDestinationCanScroll,
   438	        portraitBottomNavHeightPx
   439	    ) {
   440	        object : NestedScrollConnection {
   441	            override fun onPostScroll(
   442	                consumed: Offset,
   443	                available: Offset,
   444	                source: NestedScrollSource
   445	            ): Offset {
   446	                if (
   447	                    isPortrait &&
   448	                    usesScrollLinkedPortraitBottomNav &&
   449	                    primaryDestinationCanScroll == true &&
   450	                    consumed.y != 0f
   451	                ) {
   452	                    portraitBottomNavHiddenFraction = (
   453	                        portraitBottomNavHiddenFraction +
   454	                            consumed.y * PORTRAIT_BOTTOM_NAV_SCROLL_MULTIPLIER /
   455	                            portraitBottomNavHeightPx
   456	                        ).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
   457	                }
   458	                return Offset.Zero
   459	            }
   460	        }
   461	    }
   462	
   463	    LaunchedEffect(destination, isPortrait, primaryDestinationCanScroll) {
   464	        portraitBottomNavHiddenFraction = if (
   465	            isPortrait &&
   466	            usesScrollLinkedPortraitBottomNav &&
   467	            primaryDestinationCanScroll != false
   468	        ) {
   469	            1f
   470	        } else {
   471	            0f
   472	        }
   473	    }
   474	    val playbackSessionController = remember(userInitiatedPlaybackActive) {
   475	        CinerificPlaybackSessionController(
   476	            isUserInitiatedPlaybackActive = userInitiatedPlaybackActive,
   477	            onUserInitiatedPlaybackStarted = {
   478	                userInitiatedPlaybackActive = true
   479	            },
   480	            finishUserInitiatedPlayback = {
   481	                userInitiatedPlaybackActive = false
   482	                lastInteractionMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   483	            }
   484	        )
   485	    }
   486	    val catalogRoute = remember(
   487	        catalogRouteDestinationName,
   488	        catalogRouteGenreName,
   489	        catalogRouteModeName
   490	    ) {
   491	        cinerificSavedCatalogRoute(
   492	            destinationName = catalogRouteDestinationName,
   493	            genreName = catalogRouteGenreName,
   494	            modeName = catalogRouteModeName
   495	        )
   496	    }
   497	    val mostFrequentlyViewedProgramTitle = remember(
   498	        programPlayCounts,
   499	        mostRecentlyPlayedTitle
   500	    ) {
   501	        val highestPlayCount = programPlayCounts.values.maxOrNull()
   502	        if (highestPlayCount == null) {
   503	            null
   504	        } else {
   505	            mostRecentlyPlayedTitle?.takeIf { title ->
   506	                programPlayCounts[title] == highestPlayCount
   507	            } ?: programPlayCounts.entries.firstOrNull { (_, count) ->
   508	                count == highestPlayCount
   509	            }?.key
   510	        }
   511	    }
   512	    val mostFrequentlyViewedPlayCount = mostFrequentlyViewedProgramTitle?.let { title ->
   513	        programPlayCounts[title]
   514	    }
   515	    val longestPlaytimeProgramTitle = remember(
   516	        programPlaytimeMillis,
   517	        mostRecentlyPlayedTitle
   518	    ) {
   519	        val longestDuration = programPlaytimeMillis.values.maxOrNull()
   520	        if (longestDuration == null) {
   521	            null
   522	        } else {
   523	            mostRecentlyPlayedTitle?.takeIf { title ->
   524	                programPlaytimeMillis[title] == longestDuration
   525	            } ?: programPlaytimeMillis.entries.firstOrNull { (_, duration) ->
   526	                duration == longestDuration
   527	            }?.key
   528	        }
   529	    }
   530	    val longestPlaytimeMillis = longestPlaytimeProgramTitle?.let { title ->
   531	        programPlaytimeMillis[title]
   532	    }
   533	
   534	    LaunchedEffect(activePlaybackTitle) {
   535	        val title = activePlaybackTitle ?: return@LaunchedEffect
   536	        while (activePlaybackTitle == title) {
   537	            delay(1_000L)
   538	            if (activePlaybackTitle != title) break
   539	            val now = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
   540	            val elapsedMillis = (now - activePlaybackLastTickMillis).coerceAtLeast(0L)
   541	            activePlaybackLastTickMillis = now
   542	            val updatedDuration = (programPlaytimeMillis[title] ?: 0L) + elapsedMillis
   543	            programPlaytimeMillis = programPlaytimeMillis + (title to updatedDuration)
   544	            playbackStatsPreferences.edit()
   545	                .putLong(PLAYBACK_TIME_KEY_PREFIX + title, updatedDuration)
   546	                .apply()
   547	        }
   548	    }
   549	
   550	    fun clearCatalogRoute() {
   551	        catalogRouteDestinationName = ""
   552	        catalogRouteGenreName = ""
   553	        catalogRouteModeName = ""
   554	    }
   555	
   556	    LaunchedEffect(autoLogoutEnabled, userInitiatedPlaybackActive, lastInteractionMillis) {
   557	        if (!autoLogoutEnabled || userInitiatedPlaybackActive) return@LaunchedEffect
   558	        delay(AUTO_LOGOUT_TIMEOUT_MS)
   559	        val inactiveForMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - lastInteractionMillis
   560	        if (
   561	            autoLogoutEnabled &&
   562	            !userInitiatedPlaybackActive &&
   563	            inactiveForMillis >= AUTO_LOGOUT_TIMEOUT_MS
   564	        ) {
   565	            onSignOut()
   566	        }
   567	    }
   568	
   569	    fun showProgramDetails(title: String) {
   570	        if (destination != CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails) {
   571	            detailOriginDestination = destination
   572	        }
   573	        selectedProgramTitle = title
   574	        destination = CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails
   575	    }
   576	
   577	    fun showCatalog(route: CinerificCatalogRoute) {
   578	        catalogRouteDestinationName = route.destination.name
   579	        catalogRouteGenreName = route.genre.name
   580	        catalogRouteModeName = route.mode.name
   581	        destination = route.destination
   582	    }
   583	
   584	    fun rateProgram(title: String, rating: Int) {
   585	        userProgramRatings = userProgramRatings + (title to rating.coerceIn(1, 5))
   586	    }
   587	
   588	    fun recordProgramPlay(title: String) {
   589	        val updatedCount = (programPlayCounts[title] ?: 0) + 1
   590	        programPlayCounts = programPlayCounts + (title to updatedCount)
   591	        mostRecentlyPlayedTitle = title
   592	        playbackStatsPreferences.edit()
   593	            .putInt(PLAYBACK_COUNT_KEY_PREFIX + title, updatedCount)
   594	            .putString(MOST_RECENTLY_PLAYED_TITLE_KEY, title)
   595	            .apply()
   596	    }
   597	
   598	    fun startProgramPlaybackTimer(title: String) {
   599	        activePlaybackTitle = title
   600	        activePlaybackLastTickMillis = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
   601	    }
   602	
   603	    fun stopProgramPlaybackTimer(title: String) {
   604	        if (activePlaybackTitle != title) return
   605	        val now = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
   606	        val elapsedMillis = (now - activePlaybackLastTickMillis).coerceAtLeast(0L)
   607	        val updatedDuration = (programPlaytimeMillis[title] ?: 0L) + elapsedMillis
   608	        programPlaytimeMillis = programPlaytimeMillis + (title to updatedDuration)
   609	        playbackStatsPreferences.edit()
   610	            .putLong(PLAYBACK_TIME_KEY_PREFIX + title, updatedDuration)
   611	            .apply()
   612	        activePlaybackTitle = null
   613	        activePlaybackLastTickMillis = 0L
   614	    }
   615	
   616	    fun toggleFavoriteProgram(title: String) {
   617	        if (title in favoriteProgramTitles) {
   618	            favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles - title
   619	            return
   620	        }
   621	
   622	        val isShow = cinerificProgramIsShow(title)
   623	        val filledSlots = cinerificFilledFavoritePlaceholderCount(
   624	            titles = favoriteProgramTitles,
   625	            isShow = isShow
   626	        )
   627	        if (filledSlots >= CINERIFIC_FAVORITES_CAPACITY) {
   628	            favoritesFullPromptRequestId += 1
   629	            return
   630	        }
   631	
   632	        favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles + title
   633	    }
   634	
   635	    fun requestSignOut() {
   636	        if (signOutInProgress) return
   637	        showSignOutConfirmation = true
   638	    }
   639	
   640	    fun confirmSignOut() {
   641	        if (signOutInProgress) return
   642	        showSignOutConfirmation = false
   643	        signOutInProgress = true
   644	        onSignOutLoadingStarted()
   645	        scope.launch {
   646	            delay(SIGN_OUT_LOADING_MS)
   647	            onSignOut()
   648	        }
   649	    }
   650	
   651	    BackHandler {
   652	        when {
   653	            signOutInProgress -> Unit
   654	            showSignOutConfirmation -> showSignOutConfirmation = false
   655	            destination == CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails -> {
   656	                destination = detailOriginDestination
   657	            }
   658	            destination != CinerificDestination.Home -> {
   659	                clearCatalogRoute()
   660	                destination = CinerificDestination.Home
   661	            }
   662	            else -> requestSignOut()
   663	        }
   664	    }
   665	
   666	    CompositionLocalProvider(LocalCinerificPlaybackSessionController provides playbackSessionController) {
   667	        Box(
   668	            modifier = Modifier
   669	                .fillMaxSize()
   670	                .nestedScroll(portraitBottomNavScrollConnection)
   671	                .pointerInput(Unit) {
   672	                    awaitPointerEventScope {
   673	                        while (true) {
   674	                            awaitPointerEvent(PointerEventPass.Initial)
   675	                            lastInteractionMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   676	                        }
   677	                    }
   678	                }
   679	        ) {
   680	            destinationStateHolder.SaveableStateProvider(destination) {
   681	                when (destination) {
   682	                    CinerificDestination.Home -> CinerificHomeScreen(
   683	                        onProgramSelected = ::showProgramDetails,
   684	                        onCatalogSelected = ::showCatalog,
   685	                        onVerticalScrollabilityChanged = { primaryDestinationCanScroll = it },
   686	                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   687	                    )
   688	                    CinerificDestination.Movies,
   689	                    CinerificDestination.Shows,
   690	                    CinerificDestination.Favorites,
   691	                    CinerificDestination.Settings -> CinerificDestinationScreen(
   692	                        destination = destination,
   693	                        signedInProfile = signedInProfile,
   694	                        selectedLanguage = selectedLanguage,
   695	                        onLanguageSelected = onLanguageSelected,
   696	                        autoLogoutEnabled = autoLogoutEnabled,
   697	                        onAutoLogoutEnabledChange = { enabled ->
   698	                            autoLogoutEnabled = enabled
   699	                            lastInteractionMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   700	                        },
   701	                        onSignOut = ::requestSignOut,
   702	                        favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles,
   703	                        onFavoriteToggled = ::toggleFavoriteProgram,
   704	                        userProgramRatings = userProgramRatings,
   705	                        onProgramRated = ::rateProgram,
   706	                        mostFrequentlyViewedProgramTitle = mostFrequentlyViewedProgramTitle,
   707	                        mostFrequentlyViewedPlayCount = mostFrequentlyViewedPlayCount,
   708	                        longestPlaytimeProgramTitle = longestPlaytimeProgramTitle,
   709	                        longestPlaytimeMillis = longestPlaytimeMillis,
   710	                        programPlaytimeMillis = programPlaytimeMillis,
   711	                        mostUsedDeviceName = mostUsedDeviceName,
   712	                        onProgramSelected = ::showProgramDetails,
   713	                        catalogRoute = catalogRoute?.takeIf { it.destination == destination },
   714	                        onVerticalScrollabilityChanged = { primaryDestinationCanScroll = it },
   715	                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   716	                    )
   717	                    CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails -> CinerificProgramDetailsScreen(
   718	                        programTitle = selectedProgramTitle,
   719	                        favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles,
   720	                        onFavoriteToggled = ::toggleFavoriteProgram,
   721	                        userProgramRatings = userProgramRatings,
   722	                        onProgramRated = ::rateProgram,
   723	                        onProgramPlayed = ::recordProgramPlay,
   724	                        onProgramPlaybackStarted = ::startProgramPlaybackTimer,
   725	                        onProgramPlaybackStopped = ::stopProgramPlaybackTimer,
   726	                        onProgramSelected = ::showProgramDetails,
   727	                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   728	                    )
   729	                }
   730	            }
   731	
   732	            CinerificRightSideNavBar(
   733	                currentDestination = destination,
   734	                portraitHiddenFraction = portraitBottomNavHiddenFraction,
   735	                onPortraitBarHeightChanged = { heightPx ->
   736	                    portraitBottomNavHeightPx = heightPx.coerceAtLeast(1f)
   737	                },
   738	                onDestinationSelected = {
   739	                    clearCatalogRoute()
   740	                    destination = it
   741	                },
   742	                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   743	            )
   744	
   745	            FavoritesFullPromptOverlay(
   746	                requestId = favoritesFullPromptRequestId,
   747	                currentDestination = destination,
   748	                suppressed = userInitiatedPlaybackActive,
   749	                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   750	            )
   751	
   752	            if (showSignOutConfirmation) {
   753	                SignOutConfirmationOverlay(
   754	                    onConfirm = ::confirmSignOut,
   755	                    onDismiss = { showSignOutConfirmation = false },
   756	                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   757	                )
   758	            }
   759	        }
   760	    }
   761	}

   782	@Composable
   783	private fun SignOutConfirmationOverlay(
   784	    onConfirm: () -> Unit,
   785	    onDismiss: () -> Unit,
   786	    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
   787	) {
   788	    val frameShape = RoundedCornerShape(15.dp)
   789	    val panelShape = RoundedCornerShape(8.dp)
   790	    val frameStrokeWidth = 2.dp
   791	    val frameClearSpace = frameStrokeWidth * (10f / 3f)
   792	    val selectionStrokeAlpha = rememberCinerificSelectionStrokeAlpha()
   793	    val selectionStrokeColor = lerpColor(
   794	        Color(0xFFC86BE0),
   795	        Color(0xFFE7E7E7),
   796	        ((selectionStrokeAlpha - 0.5f) / 0.17f).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
   797	    )
   798	
   799	    Box(
   800	        modifier = modifier,
   801	        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
   802	    ) {
   803	        Box(
   804	            modifier = Modifier
   805	                .fillMaxSize()
   806	                .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.72f))
   807	                .clickable(onClick = {})
   808	        )
   809	        Box(
   810	            modifier = Modifier
   811	                .fillMaxWidth(0.84f)
   812	                .widthIn(max = 420.dp)
   813	                .border(
   814	                    frameStrokeWidth,
   815	                    selectionStrokeColor,
   816	                    frameShape
   817	                )
   818	                .padding(frameClearSpace)
   819	        ) {
   820	            Column(
   821	                modifier = Modifier
   822	                    .fillMaxWidth()
   823	                    .clip(panelShape)
   824	                    .background(Color(0xFF252126))
   825	                    .padding(horizontal = 24.dp, vertical = 22.dp),
   826	                horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
   827	                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(20.dp)
   828	            ) {
   829	                Text(
   830	                    text = "Sign out from\nCinerific?",
   831	                    color = Color.White,
   832	                    fontFamily = CinerificAppTextFontFamily,
   833	                    fontSize = 26.sp,
   834	                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
   835	                    lineHeight = 34.sp,
   836	                    letterSpacing = 0.55.sp,
   837	                    textAlign = TextAlign.Center
   838	                )
   839	                Row(
   840	                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
   841	                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp)
   842	                ) {
   843	                    SignOutConfirmationButton(
   844	                        label = "Yes",
   845	                        backgroundColor = ColorFrame2Background,
   846	                        contentColor = Color.White,
   847	                        borderColor = Color(0xFFC86BE0),
   848	                        onClick = onConfirm,
   849	                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
   850	                    )
   851	                    SignOutConfirmationButton(
   852	                        label = "No",
   853	                        backgroundColor = Color(0xFF3A343C),
   854	                        contentColor = Color.White,
   855	                        borderColor = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.72f),
   856	                        onClick = onDismiss,
   857	                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
   858	                    )
   859	                }
   860	            }
   861	        }
   862	    }
   863	}

   865	@Composable
   866	private fun SignOutConfirmationButton(
   867	    label: String,
   868	    backgroundColor: Color,
   869	    contentColor: Color,
   870	    borderColor: Color,
   871	    onClick: () -> Unit,
   872	    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
   873	) {

   881	            .clickable(onClick = onClick),
   882	        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
   883	    ) {
   884	        Text(
   885	            text = label,
   886	            color = contentColor,
   887	            fontFamily = CinerificAppTextFontFamily,

   894	
   895	private fun cinerificSavedCatalogRoute(
   896	    destinationName: String,
   897	    genreName: String,
   898	    modeName: String
   899	): CinerificCatalogRoute? {
   900	    if (destinationName.isBlank() || genreName.isBlank() || modeName.isBlank()) return null
   901	
   902	    val destination = CinerificDestination.values().firstOrNull { it.name == destinationName } ?: return null
   903	    val genre = ViewportGenre.values().firstOrNull { it.name == genreName } ?: return null
   904	    val mode = ViewportMode.values().firstOrNull { it.name == modeName } ?: return null
   905	
   906	    return CinerificCatalogRoute(
   907	        destination = destination,
   908	        genre = genre,
   909	        mode = mode
   910	    )
   911	}
   912	
   913	@Composable
   914	private fun FavoritesFullPromptOverlay(
   915	    requestId: Int,
   916	    currentDestination: CinerificDestination,
   917	    suppressed: Boolean,
   918	    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
   919	) {
   920	    var visible by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
   921	
   922	    LaunchedEffect(requestId, suppressed) {
   923	        if (suppressed) {
   924	            visible = false
   925	            return@LaunchedEffect
   926	        }
   927	        if (requestId == 0) {
   928	            visible = false
   929	            return@LaunchedEffect
   930	        }
   931	        visible = true
   932	        delay(FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_VISIBLE_MS)
   933	        visible = false
   934	    }
   935	
   936	    val alpha by animateFloatAsState(
   937	        targetValue = if (visible) 1f else 0f,
   938	        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = if (visible) 140 else 190),
   939	        label = "favorites-full-prompt-alpha"
   940	    )
   941	    val promptScale by animateFloatAsState(
   942	        targetValue = if (visible) 1f else 0.94f,
   943	        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = if (visible) 140 else 190),
   944	        label = "favorites-full-prompt-scale"
   945	    )
   946	
   947	    if (suppressed || requestId == 0 || (!visible && alpha <= 0.01f)) return
   948	
   949	    BoxWithConstraints(modifier = modifier) {
   950	        val stageScale = maxWidth.value / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH
   951	        val isPortraitDetail = currentDestination == CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails &&
   952	            maxHeight > maxWidth
   953	        val promptY = if (isPortraitDetail) {
   954	            DETAIL_HERO_LOGO_CENTER_Y - FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_HEIGHT / 2f
   955	        } else {
   956	            FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_Y
   957	        }
   958	        FavoritesFullPromptBubble(
   959	            stageScale = stageScale,
   960	            modifier = Modifier
   961	                .absoluteOffset(
   962	                    x = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_X * stageScale).dp,
   963	                    y = (promptY * stageScale).dp
   964	                )
   965	                .graphicsLayer {
   966	                    this.alpha = alpha
   967	                    scaleX = promptScale
   968	                    scaleY = promptScale
   969	                    transformOrigin = TransformOrigin(0.5f, 0f)
   970	                }
   971	        )
   972	    }
   973	}
   974	
   975	@Composable
   976	private fun FavoritesFullPromptBubble(
   977	    stageScale: Float,
   978	    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
   979	) {
   980	    Box(
   981	        modifier = modifier.requiredSize(
   982	            width = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH * stageScale).dp,
   983	            height = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_HEIGHT * stageScale).dp
   984	        )
   985	    ) {
   986	        Canvas(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
   987	            val xScale = size.width / FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH
   988	            val yScale = size.height / FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_HEIGHT
   989	            val pointer = Path().apply {
   990	                moveTo(47.5f * xScale, 0f)
   991	                lineTo(63f * xScale, FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale)
   992	                lineTo(32f * xScale, FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale)
   993	                close()
   994	            }
   995	            drawPath(pointer, ColorFavoritesFullPromptAccent)
   996	            drawRoundRect(
   997	                color = ColorFavoritesFullPromptFill,
   998	                topLeft = Offset(0f, FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale),
   999	                size = Size(
  1000	                    width = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH * xScale,
  1001	                    height = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_HEIGHT * yScale
  1002	                ),
  1003	                cornerRadius = CornerRadius(
  1004	                    x = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * xScale,
  1005	                    y = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * yScale
  1006	                )
  1007	            )
  1008	            drawRoundRect(
  1009	                color = ColorFavoritesFullPromptAccent,
  1010	                topLeft = Offset(
  1011	                    x = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE * xScale / 2f,
  1012	                    y = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale +
  1013	                        FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE * yScale / 2f
  1014	                ),
  1015	                size = Size(
  1016	                    width = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH - FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE) * xScale,
  1017	                    height = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_HEIGHT - FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE) * yScale
  1018	                ),
  1019	                cornerRadius = CornerRadius(
  1020	                    x = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * xScale,
  1021	                    y = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * yScale
  1022	                ),
  1023	                style = Stroke(width = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE * min(xScale, yScale))
  1024	            )
  1025	        }
  1026	        Text(
  1027	            text = "You've\nfilled\nup on\nfavorites!",
  1028	            color = ColorFavoritesFullPromptAccent,
  1029	            fontFamily = CinerificAppTextFontFamily,
  1030	            fontSize = (14f * stageScale).sp,
  1031	            fontWeight = FontWeight.Black,
  1032	            lineHeight = (21.252f * stageScale).sp,
  1033	            textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
  1034	            modifier = Modifier
  1035	                .absoluteOffset(
  1036	                    x = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_X * stageScale).dp,
  1037	                    y = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_Y * stageScale).dp
  1038	                )
  1039	                .requiredSize(
  1040	                    width = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_WIDTH * stageScale).dp,
  1041	                    height = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_HEIGHT * stageScale).dp
  1042	                )
  1043	        )
  1044	    }
  1045	}

  1047	@Composable
  1048	private fun BootIntroFromFigma(progress: Float) {
  1049	    val p = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1050	    val images = rememberIntroImages()
  1051	    val blackToPurple = linearSegmentMs(
  1052	        p,
  1053	        SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS,
  1054	        SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS + SCREEN_PINK_MS
  1055	    )
  1056	    val purpleToSettle = easedSegmentMs(p, 2500, 3900)
  1057	    val background = introBackgroundBrush(
  1058	        solidProgress = blackToPurple,
  1059	        gradientProgress = purpleToSettle
  1060	    )
  1061	    val logoAlpha = linearSegmentMs(p, 90, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS)
  1062	    val logoEntryProgress = bouncySegmentMs(p, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS, LOGO_ENTRY_END_MS)
  1063	    val logoFinalProgress = easedSegmentMs(p, FINAL_SETTLE_START_MS, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
  1064	    val avatarSettleAlpha = easedSegmentMs(p, 2300, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
  1065	    val avatarYOffset = lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarSettleAlpha)
  1066	
  1067	    Canvas(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
  1068	        val stageScale = min(size.width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, size.height / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
  1069	        val stageLeft = (size.width - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stageScale) / 2f
  1070	        val stageTop = (size.height - FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT * stageScale) / 2f
  1071	        val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY(size.width > size.height)
  1072	
  1073	        drawRect(brush = background, size = size)
  1074	
  1075	        if (logoAlpha > 0.01f) {
  1076	            val baseBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 222f, y = 150f, w = 750f, h = 535f)
  1077	            val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(
  1078	                start = FigmaBounds(x = 447f, y = -417f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
  1079	                end = baseBounds,
  1080	                amount = logoEntryProgress
  1081	            )
  1082	            val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(
  1083	                start = FigmaBounds(x = 453f, y = 1037f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
  1084	                end = baseBounds,
  1085	                amount = logoEntryProgress
  1086	            )
  1087	            val finalBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 297f, y = 11f, w = 600f, h = 428f)
  1088	
  1089	            drawFigmaImage(
  1090	                image = images.logoSimple,
  1091	                bounds = lerpBounds(simpleEntry, finalBounds, logoFinalProgress),
  1092	                stageLeft = stageLeft,
  1093	                stageTop = stageTop,
  1094	                stageScale = stageScale,
  1095	                alpha = logoAlpha
  1096	            )
  1097	            drawFigmaImage(
  1098	                image = images.logoEyes,
  1099	                bounds = lerpBounds(eyesEntry, finalBounds, logoFinalProgress),
  1100	                stageLeft = stageLeft,
  1101	                stageTop = stageTop,
  1102	                stageScale = stageScale,
  1103	                alpha = logoAlpha
  1104	            )
  1105	        }
  1106	
  1107	        if (avatarSettleAlpha > 0.01f) {
  1108	            val y = avatarYOffset
  1109	            drawFigmaImage(images.steveAvatar, FigmaBounds(152f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
  1110	            drawFigmaImage(images.martinAvatar, FigmaBounds(390f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
  1111	            drawFigmaImage(images.jannyAvatar, FigmaBounds(628f, 454f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
  1112	            drawFigmaImage(images.guestAvatar, FigmaBounds(866f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
  1113	
  1114	            drawFigmaImage(images.steveName, FigmaBounds(130f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
  1115	            drawFigmaImage(images.martinName, FigmaBounds(368f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
  1116	            drawFigmaImage(images.jannyName, FigmaBounds(606f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
  1117	            drawFigmaImage(images.guestName, FigmaBounds(854f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 200f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
  1118	        }
  1119	    }
  1120	}

  1314	private fun DrawScope.drawFigmaImage(
  1315	    image: ImageBitmap,
  1316	    bounds: FigmaBounds,
  1317	    stageLeft: Float,
  1318	    stageTop: Float,
  1319	    stageScale: Float,
  1320	    alpha: Float = 1f,
  1321	    clipCircle: Boolean = false
  1322	) {
  1323	    val left = stageLeft + bounds.x * stageScale
  1324	    val top = stageTop + bounds.y * stageScale
  1325	    val width = bounds.w * stageScale
  1326	    val height = bounds.h * stageScale
  1327	
  1328	    if (clipCircle) {
  1329	        val clip = Path().apply {
  1330	            addOval(Rect(left, top, left + width, top + height))
  1331	        }
  1332	        clipPath(clip) {
  1333	            drawImageBitmap(image, left, top, width, height, alpha)
  1334	        }
  1335	    } else {
  1336	        drawImageBitmap(image, left, top, width, height, alpha)
  1337	    }
  1338	}
  1339	
  1340	private fun DrawScope.drawImageBitmap(
  1341	    image: ImageBitmap,
  1342	    left: Float,
  1343	    top: Float,
  1344	    width: Float,
  1345	    height: Float,
  1346	    alpha: Float
  1347	) {
  1348	    drawImage(
  1349	        image = image,
  1350	        dstOffset = IntOffset(left.roundToInt(), top.roundToInt()),
  1351	        dstSize = IntSize(width.roundToInt(), height.roundToInt()),
  1352	        alpha = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1353	    )
  1354	}

  1356	@Composable
  1357	private fun SettlingLogoLayer(
  1358	    scale: Float,
  1359	    entryProgress: Float,
  1360	    finalProgress: Float,
  1361	    alpha: Float
  1362	) {
  1363	    val baseBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 222f, y = 150f, w = 750f, h = 535f)
  1364	    val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(
  1365	        start = FigmaBounds(x = 447f, y = 1037f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
  1366	        end = baseBounds,
  1367	        amount = entryProgress
  1368	    )
  1369	    val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(
  1370	        start = FigmaBounds(x = 453f, y = -417f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
  1371	        end = baseBounds,
  1372	        amount = entryProgress
  1373	    )
  1374	    val finalBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 297f, y = 11f, w = 600f, h = 428f)
  1375	    val simpleBounds = lerpBounds(simpleEntry, finalBounds, finalProgress)
  1376	    val eyesBounds = lerpBounds(eyesEntry, finalBounds, finalProgress)
  1377	
  1378	    TransformedFigmaAssetImage(
  1379	        base = baseBounds,
  1380	        target = simpleBounds,
  1381	        scale = scale,
  1382	        resId = R.drawable.logo_simple_large,
  1383	        alpha = alpha
  1384	    )
  1385	    TransformedFigmaAssetImage(
  1386	        base = baseBounds,
  1387	        target = eyesBounds,
  1388	        scale = scale,
  1389	        resId = R.drawable.logo_eyes_large,
  1390	        alpha = alpha
  1391	    )
  1392	}

  1450	@Composable
  1451	private fun SignInPromptText(
  1452	    text: String,
  1453	    x: Float,
  1454	    y: Float,
  1455	    textSize: Float,
  1456	    fontWeight: FontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
  1457	    textAlign: TextAlign = TextAlign.Center,
  1458	    scale: Float
  1459	) {
  1460	    val contentAlignment = when (textAlign) {
  1461	        TextAlign.Left -> Alignment.CenterStart
  1462	        TextAlign.Right -> Alignment.CenterEnd
  1463	        else -> Alignment.Center
  1464	    }
  1465	    Box(
  1466	        modifier = Modifier
  1467	            .absoluteOffset(
  1468	                x = figma(x, scale),
  1469	                y = figma(y, scale)
  1470	            )
  1471	            .requiredSize(
  1472	                width = figma(ACCOUNT_PROMPT_WIDTH, scale),
  1473	                height = figma(ACCOUNT_PROMPT_HEIGHT, scale)
  1474	            ),
  1475	        contentAlignment = contentAlignment
  1476	    ) {
  1477	        Text(
  1478	            text = text,
  1479	            color = Color.White,
  1480	            fontFamily = CinerificAppTextFontFamily,
  1481	            fontSize = (textSize * scale).sp,
  1482	            fontWeight = fontWeight,
  1483	            lineHeight = (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_LINE_HEIGHT * textSize / ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE * scale).sp,
  1484	            maxLines = 1,
  1485	            softWrap = false,
  1486	            textAlign = textAlign
  1487	        )
  1488	    }
  1489	}

  1551	@Composable
  1552	private fun TransformedFigmaAssetImage(
  1553	    base: FigmaBounds,
  1554	    target: FigmaBounds,
  1555	    scale: Float,
  1556	    @DrawableRes resId: Int,
  1557	    alpha: Float = 1f
  1558	) {
  1559	    val density = LocalDensity.current.density
  1560	    Image(
  1561	        painter = painterResource(id = resId),
  1562	        contentDescription = null,
  1563	        contentScale = ContentScale.FillBounds,
  1564	        modifier = Modifier
  1565	            .absoluteOffset(x = figma(base.x, scale), y = figma(base.y, scale))
  1566	            .requiredSize(figma(base.w, scale), figma(base.h, scale))
  1567	            .graphicsLayer {
  1568	                this.alpha = alpha
  1569	                transformOrigin = TransformOrigin(0f, 0f)
  1570	                translationX = (target.x - base.x) * scale * density
  1571	                translationY = (target.y - base.y) * scale * density
  1572	                scaleX = target.w / base.w
  1573	                scaleY = target.h / base.h
  1574	            }
  1575	    )
  1576	}
  1577	
  1578	@Composable
  1579	private fun FigmaAssetImage(
  1580	    x: Float,
  1581	    y: Float,
  1582	    w: Float,
  1583	    h: Float,
  1584	    scale: Float,
  1585	    @DrawableRes resId: Int,
  1586	    alpha: Float = 1f
  1587	) {
  1588	    Image(
  1589	        painter = painterResource(id = resId),
  1590	        contentDescription = null,
  1591	        contentScale = ContentScale.FillBounds,
  1592	        modifier = Modifier
  1593	            .absoluteOffset(x = figma(x, scale), y = figma(y, scale))
  1594	            .requiredSize(figma(w, scale), figma(h, scale))
  1595	            .graphicsLayer(alpha = alpha)
  1596	    )
  1597	}

  1599	@Composable
  1600	private fun FigmaStage(background: Brush, content: @Composable BoxWithConstraintsScope.(Float) -> Unit) {
  1601	    BoxWithConstraints(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
  1602	        val outerMaxWidth = this.maxWidth
  1603	        val outerMaxHeight = this.maxHeight
  1604	        val scale = min(outerMaxWidth.value / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, outerMaxHeight.value / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
  1605	        val stageWidth = figma(FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, scale)
  1606	        val stageHeight = figma(FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT, scale)
  1607	
  1608	        Box(
  1609	            modifier = Modifier
  1610	                .fillMaxSize()
  1611	                .background(background)
  1612	        ) {
  1613	            BoxWithConstraints(
  1614	                modifier = Modifier
  1615	                    .align(Alignment.Center)
  1616	                    .requiredSize(stageWidth, stageHeight)
  1617	            ) {
  1618	                content(scale)
  1619	            }
  1620	        }
  1621	    }
  1622	}
  1623	
  1624	private fun figma(px: Float, scale: Float) = (px * scale).dp
  1625	
  1626	private fun signInStackShiftY(isLandscape: Boolean): Float {
  1627	    return if (isLandscape) {
  1628	        SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_STACK_SHIFT_Y
  1629	    } else {
  1630	        SIGN_IN_PORTRAIT_STACK_SHIFT_Y
  1631	    }
  1632	}
  1633	
  1634	private fun signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY(isLandscape: Boolean): Float {
  1635	    return if (isLandscape) SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_ACCOUNT_PROMPT_EXTRA_SHIFT_Y else 0f
  1636	}
  1637	
  1638	private fun signInAccountPromptTextSize(isLandscape: Boolean): Float {
  1639	    return if (isLandscape) ACCOUNT_PROMPT_LANDSCAPE_TEXT_SIZE else ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE
  1640	}
  1641	
  1642	private fun introBackgroundBrush(solidProgress: Float, gradientProgress: Float): Brush {
  1643	    val solid = lerpColor(ColorFrame1Background, ColorFrame2Background, solidProgress)
  1644	    return Brush.verticalGradient(
  1645	        colors = listOf(
  1646	            lerpColor(solid, ColorIntroGradientTop, gradientProgress),
  1647	            lerpColor(solid, ColorIntroGradientCenter, gradientProgress),
  1648	            lerpColor(solid, ColorIntroGradientBottom, gradientProgress)
  1649	        )
  1650	    )
  1651	}
  1652	
  1653	private fun solidBrush(color: Color): Brush = Brush.verticalGradient(
  1654	    colors = listOf(color, color)
  1655	)
  1656	
  1657	private fun bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis: Long): Float {
  1658	    val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - bootStartMillis
  1659	    return (elapsed / BOOT_ANIMATION_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1660	}
  1661	
  1662	private fun easedSegment(value: Float, start: Float, end: Float): Float {
  1663	    val progress = ((value - start) / (end - start)).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1664	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(progress)
  1665	}
  1666	
  1667	private fun linearSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  1668	    val ms = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * BOOT_ANIMATION_MS
  1669	    return ((ms - startMs) / (endMs - startMs).toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1670	}
  1671	
  1672	private fun easedSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  1673	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  1674	}
  1675	
  1676	private fun bouncySegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  1677	    return bouncyEasing(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  1678	}
  1679	
  1680	private fun bouncyEasing(value: Float): Float {
  1681	    val t = value.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1682	    val base = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(t)
  1683	    val bounce = sin(t * PI.toFloat() * 4.5f) * (1f - t) * 0.13f
  1684	    return base + bounce
  1685	}
  1686	
  1687	private fun lerpFloat(start: Float, end: Float, amount: Float) = start + (end - start) * amount
  1688	
  1689	private fun lerpBounds(start: FigmaBounds, end: FigmaBounds, amount: Float) = FigmaBounds(
  1690	    x = lerpFloat(start.x, end.x, amount),
  1691	    y = lerpFloat(start.y, end.y, amount),
  1692	    w = lerpFloat(start.w, end.w, amount),
  1693	    h = lerpFloat(start.h, end.h, amount)
  1694	)
  1695	
  1696	private val CinerificIntroSnapshotSaver = listSaver<CinerificIntroSnapshot, Any>(
  1697	    save = { snapshot ->
  1698	        listOf(
  1699	            snapshot.activeFlowName,
  1700	            snapshot.usernameText,
  1701	            snapshot.passwordText,
  1702	            snapshot.confirmPasswordText,

  1710	            snapshot.rememberMeChecked,
  1711	            snapshot.createAvatarIndex,

  1718	            usernameText = values.getOrNull(1) as? String ?: "",
  1719	            passwordText = values.getOrNull(2) as? String ?: "",
  1720	            confirmPasswordText = values.getOrNull(3) as? String ?: "",
  1721	            emailText = values.getOrNull(4) as? String ?: "",
  1722	            monthText = values.getOrNull(5) as? String ?: "Mon",
  1723	            dayText = values.getOrNull(6) as? String ?: "Day",
  1724	            yearText = values.getOrNull(7) as? String ?: "Year",
  1725	            subscriptionTierText = values.getOrNull(8) as? String ?: "Subscription Tier",
  1726	            forgotRecoveryTargetName = values.getOrNull(9) as? String ?: "Password",
  1727	            forgotPasswordSubmissionStateName = values.getOrNull(10) as? String ?: "Idle",
  1728	            rememberMeChecked = values.getOrNull(11) as? Boolean ?: false,

   351	        letterSpacing = MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_LETTER_SPACING
   352	    }

   487	    fun handleSystemBack(): Boolean {
   488	        if (activeMockFlow == null || mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress <= 0f) return false
   489	        closeMockSignInScreen()
   490	        return true
   491	    }
   492	
   493	    fun restoreIntroSnapshot(snapshot: CinerificIntroSnapshot) {
   494	        if (appliedIntroSnapshot == snapshot) return
   495	
   496	        val restoredFlow = enumValueOrNull<MockAccountFlow>(snapshot.activeFlowName)
   497	        val restoredRecoveryTarget = enumValueOrNull<MockForgotRecoveryTarget>(
   498	            snapshot.forgotRecoveryTargetName
   499	        ) ?: MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Password
   500	        val restoredSubmissionState = enumValueOrNull<MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState>(
   501	            snapshot.forgotPasswordSubmissionStateName
   502	        ) ?: MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Idle
   503	
   504	        pressedAvatarProfile = null
   505	        clickAvatarProfile = null
   506	        pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   507	        pressedSignInPrompt = false
   508	        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   509	        pressedMockField = null
   510	        pressedRememberMe = false
   511	        pressedBackChevron = false
   512	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   513	        activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   514	        mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   515	        mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   516	        lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
   517	        pressedMockDropdown = null
   518	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   519	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   520	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   521	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   522	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   523	        mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   524	        activeComposingText = ""
   525	        expandedMockDropdown = null
   526	        focusedMockField = null
   527	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = null
   528	        mockInputDimActiveField = null
   529	        mockInputDimActiveDropdown = null
   530	
   531	        activeMockFlow = restoredFlow
   532	        if (restoredFlow == null) {
   533	            mockSignInStartMillis = null
   534	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 0f
   535	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 0f
   536	        } else {
   537	            mockSignInStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   538	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 1f
   539	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 1f
   540	        }
   541	
   542	        rememberMeChecked = snapshot.rememberMeChecked
   543	        usernameText = snapshot.usernameText
   544	        mockCreateUsernameRevealProgress = if (usernameText.isEmpty()) 0f else 1f
   545	        passwordText = snapshot.passwordText
   546	        confirmPasswordText = snapshot.confirmPasswordText
   547	        emailText = snapshot.emailText
   548	        monthText = snapshot.monthText
   549	        dayText = snapshot.dayText
   550	        yearText = snapshot.yearText
   551	        subscriptionTierText = normalizedMembershipTierSelection(snapshot.subscriptionTierText)
   552	        forgotRecoveryTarget = restoredRecoveryTarget
   553	        forgotPasswordSubmissionState = restoredSubmissionState
   554	        forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = if (
   555	            restoredSubmissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading
   556	        ) {
   557	            SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   558	        } else {
   559	            null
   560	        }
   561	
   562	        mockCreateAvatarIndex = snapshot.createAvatarIndex.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)
   563	        portraitProfileIndex = snapshot.portraitProfileIndex.coerceIn(0, FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.lastIndex)
   564	        resetPortraitProfileCarousel()
   565	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartMillis = null
   566	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselFromIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex
   567	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselToIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex
   568	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselDirection = MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next
   569	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartProgress = 0f
   570	        mockDropdownScrollOffsets.keys.forEach { dropdown ->
   571	            mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] = 0
   572	        }
   573	
   574	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
   575	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
   576	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   577	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   578	        appliedIntroSnapshot = currentIntroSnapshot()
   579	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   580	    }

   582	    override fun onSizeChanged(width: Int, height: Int, oldWidth: Int, oldHeight: Int) {
   583	        super.onSizeChanged(width, height, oldWidth, oldHeight)
   584	        pressedAvatarProfile = null
   585	        clickAvatarProfile = null
   586	        pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   587	        pressedSignInPrompt = false
   588	        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   589	        pressedMockField = null
   590	        pressedRememberMe = false
   591	        pressedBackChevron = false
   592	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   593	        activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   594	        mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   595	        pressedMockDropdown = null
   596	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   597	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   598	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   599	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   600	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   601	        mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   602	        resetPortraitProfileCarousel()
   603	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   604	    }
