   606	    override fun onApplyWindowInsets(insets: WindowInsets): WindowInsets {
   607	        val systemBars = WindowInsetsCompat.toWindowInsetsCompat(insets, this)
   608	            .getInsets(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.systemBars())
   609	        systemBarInsetTopPx = systemBars.top
   610	        systemBarInsetBottomPx = systemBars.bottom
   611	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   612	        return super.onApplyWindowInsets(insets)
   613	    }
   614	
   615	    override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
   616	        super.onDraw(canvas)
   617	
   618	        if (IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis == 0L) {
   619	            IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   620	        }
   621	        val progress = bootProgressAt(IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis)
   622	        val blackToPurple = linearSegmentMs(
   623	            progress,
   624	            SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS,
   625	            SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS + SCREEN_PINK_MS
   626	        )
   627	        val purpleToSettle = easedSegmentMs(progress, 2500, 3900)
   628	        drawIntroBackground(canvas, blackToPurple, purpleToSettle)
   629	
   630	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return
   631	        finishMockSignInTransitionIfNeeded()
   632	        finishMockFlowSwitchIfNeeded()
   633	        finishPortraitProfileCarouselIfNeeded()
   634	        finishMockCreateAvatarCarouselIfNeeded()
   635	        finishMockForgotPasswordSubmissionIfNeeded()
   636	        val mockProgress = mockSignInProgress()
   637	        updateMockFormFocusAnimation()
   638	        updateMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   639	        updateMockInputDimAnimation()
   640	        updateMockCreateUsernameRevealAnimation()
   641	        val formFocusMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress)
   642	
   643	        val logoAlpha = linearSegmentMs(progress, 90, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS)
   644	        if (logoAlpha > 0.01f) {
   645	            drawSettlingLogo(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, progress, logoAlpha)
   646	        }
   647	
   648	        val avatarAlpha = easedSegmentMs(progress, 2300, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   649	        val mockMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockProgress)
   650	        val createWelcomeTransition = isMockCreateWelcomeTransition()
   651	        if (avatarAlpha > 0.01f) {
   652	            val stackAlpha = avatarAlpha * (1f - mockMotion)
   653	            val y = lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarAlpha) + MOCK_SIGN_IN_STACK_EXIT_Y * mockMotion
   654	            if (!createWelcomeTransition && stackAlpha > 0.01f) {
   655	                drawWelcomeAvatarStack(canvas, stage, y, stackAlpha)
   656	            }
   657	        }
   658	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   659	            val outgoingFlow = outgoingMockFlow
   660	            if (outgoingFlow != null) {
   661	                val switchMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFlowSwitchProgress())
   662	                drawMockSignInFormForFlow(
   663	                    canvas,
   664	                    outgoingFlow,
   665	                    stage.left,
   666	                    stage.top,
   667	                    stage.scale,
   668	                    1f - switchMotion,
   669	                    formFocusMotion,
   670	                    updateLabelAnimation = false
   671	                )
   672	                activeMockFlow?.let { incomingFlow ->
   673	                    drawMockSignInFormForFlow(
   674	                        canvas,
   675	                        incomingFlow,
   676	                        stage.left,
   677	                        stage.top,
   678	                        stage.scale,
   679	                        switchMotion,
   680	                        formFocusMotion,
   681	                        updateLabelAnimation = true
   682	                    )
   683	                }
   684	            } else {
   685	                drawMockSignInForm(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, mockMotion, formFocusMotion)
   686	            }
   687	        }
   688	        drawAccountPrompt(
   689	            canvas = canvas,
   690	            stageLeft = stage.left,
   691	            stageTop = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) stage.top else accountPromptStageTop(stage.scale),
   692	            stageScale = stage.scale,
   693	            yOffset = if (mockSignInStartMillis == null) {
   694	                lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarAlpha)
   695	            } else {
   696	                0f
   697	            },
   698	            alpha = avatarAlpha
   699	        )
   700	        drawMockCreateAvatarTransitionOverlay(
   701	            canvas,
   702	            stage,
   703	            mockMotion,
   704	            formFocusMotion
   705	        )
   706	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   707	            drawPortraitStickyBottomBannerForeground(
   708	                canvas,
   709	                stage,
   710	                blackToPurple,
   711	                purpleToSettle
   712	            )
   713	            drawBackChevron(
   714	                canvas,
   715	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X,
   716	                mockBackChevronRowTopY(),
   717	                stage.left,
   718	                if (usesPortraitStackedAuthLayout()) {
   719	                    stage.top
   720	                } else {
   721	                    accountPromptStageTop(stage.scale)
   722	                },
   723	                stage.scale,
   724	                mockMotion
   725	            )
   726	        }
   727	
   728	        if (
   729	            progress < 1f ||
   730	            isMockSignInAnimating() ||
   731	            isMockFlowSwitchAnimating() ||
   732	            isMockFormLabelAnimating() ||
   733	            isMockFormFocusAnimating() ||
   734	            isMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimating() ||
   735	            isPortraitProfileCarouselAnimating() ||
   736	            isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating() ||
   737	            isMockForgotPasswordSubmissionAnimating() ||
   738	            isMockInputDimAnimating() ||
   739	            isMockCreateUsernameRevealAnimating()
   740	        ) {
   741	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   742	        }
   743	    }
   744	
   745	    override fun onTouchEvent(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
   746	        return when (event.actionMasked) {
   747	            MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
   748	                mockTouchDownX = event.x
   749	                mockTouchDownY = event.y
   750	                mockDragReturnEligibleAtTouchDown = false
   751	                portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
   752	                portraitProfileDragDeltaX = 0f
   753	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   754	                    mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   755	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   756	                    portraitAuthScrollStartY = event.y
   757	                    portraitAuthScrollStartOffset = portraitAuthScrollY
   758	                    portraitAuthScrollMoved = false
   759	                    if (isMockFlowSwitchAnimating()) {
   760	                        return true
   761	                    }
   762	                    mockDragReturnEligibleAtTouchDown = isMockAuthScreenAtBottom()
   763	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt = settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   764	                    pressedSignInPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   765	                        settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   766	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   767	                        !pressedSignInPrompt &&
   768	                        settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   769	                    if (
   770	                        pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   771	                        pressedSignInPrompt ||
   772	                        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt
   773	                    ) {
   774	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   775	                        return true
   776	                    }
   777	                    val touchedDropdownOptions = mockDropdownOptionsHit(event.x, event.y)
   778	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = touchedDropdownOptions?.takeIf { mockDropdownCanScroll(it) }
   779	                    mockDropdownScrollStartY = event.y
   780	                    mockDropdownScrollStartOffset = activeMockDropdownScroll?.let { mockDropdownScrollOffset(it) } ?: 0
   781	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = if (touchedDropdownOptions != null) {
   782	                        mockDropdownOptionHit(event.x, event.y)
   783	                    } else {
   784	                        null
   785	                    }
   786	                    pressedMockDropdown = if (pressedMockDropdownOption == null && touchedDropdownOptions == null) {
   787	                        mockDropdownHit(event.x, event.y)
   788	                    } else {
   789	                        null
   790	                    }
   791	                    pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = if (
   792	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   793	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   794	                        pressedMockDropdown == null
   795	                    ) {
   796	                        mockForgotRecoveryTargetHit(event.x, event.y)
   797	                    } else {
   798	                        null
   799	                    }
   800	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   801	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   802	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   803	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   804	                        mockForgotPasswordSubmitHit(event.x, event.y)
   805	                    val touchedCreateAvatarCarousel = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   806	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   807	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   808	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   809	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   810	                        mockCreateAvatarCarouselHit(event.x, event.y) &&
   811	                        !isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()
   812	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   813	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   814	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   815	                    lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
   816	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = if (
   817	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   818	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   819	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   820	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   821	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   822	                        touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   823	                    ) {
   824	                        mockCreateAvatarNavHit(event.x, event.y)
   825	                    } else {
   826	                        null
   827	                    }
   828	                    pressedMockField = if (
   829	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   830	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   831	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   832	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   833	                        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   834	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   835	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   836	                    ) {
   837	                        mockSignInFieldHit(event.x, event.y)
   838	                    } else {
   839	                        null
   840	                    }
   841	                    pressedRememberMe = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   842	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel &&
   843	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   844	                        pressedMockField == null &&
   845	                        mockRememberMeHit(event.x, event.y)
   846	                    pressedBackChevron = pressedMockField == null &&
   847	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   848	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel &&
   849	                        !pressedRememberMe &&
   850	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   851	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   852	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   853	                        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   854	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   855	                        mockBackChevronHit(event.x, event.y)
   856	                    postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   857	                    return true
   858	                }
   859	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   860	                pressedSignInPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt && settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   861	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   862	                    !pressedSignInPrompt &&
   863	                    settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   864	                pressedAvatarProfile = if (
   865	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   866	                    pressedSignInPrompt ||
   867	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
   868	                    isPortraitProfileCarouselAnimating()
   869	                ) {
   870	                    null
   871	                } else {
   872	                    settledAvatarHitProfile(event.x, event.y)
   873	                }
   874	                // This custom view owns the entire welcome surface. Returning false for a
   875	                // slightly imprecise DOWN lets AndroidView's parent cancel the rest of the
   876	                // gesture, so a valid UP never reaches the link or avatar hit test.
   877	                true
   878	            }
   879	            MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
   880	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   881	                    if (updatePortraitAuthScroll(event)) {
   882	                        return true
   883	                    }
   884	                    if (updateMockCreateAvatarDrag(event)) {
   885	                        return true
   886	                    }
   887	                    if (updateMockDropdownScroll(event)) {
   888	                        return true
   889	                    }
   890	                    if (mockDropdownOptionsHit(mockTouchDownX, mockTouchDownY) == null && shouldStartMockDragReturn(event)) {
   891	                        mockDragReturnInProgress = true
   892	                        portraitAuthScrollMoved = false
   893	                        closeMockSignInScreen()
   894	                    }
   895	                    return true
   896	                }
   897	                if (isPortraitIntroLayout() && pressedAvatarProfile != null) {
   898	                    val horizontalDelta = event.x - mockTouchDownX
   899	                    val verticalDelta = event.y - mockTouchDownY
   900	                    if (abs(horizontalDelta) > touchSlop && abs(horizontalDelta) > abs(verticalDelta)) {
   901	                        portraitProfileSwipeMoved = true
   902	                    }
   903	                    if (portraitProfileSwipeMoved) {
   904	                        portraitProfileDragDeltaX = horizontalDelta
   905	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   906	                    }
   907	                    return true
   908	                }
   909	                true
   910	            }
   911	            MotionEvent.ACTION_UP -> {
   912	                mockDragReturnEligibleAtTouchDown = false
   913	                if (portraitAuthScrollMoved) {
   914	                    portraitAuthScrollMoved = false
   915	                    pressedMockField = null
   916	                    pressedMockDropdown = null
   917	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   918	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   919	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   920	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   921	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   922	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   923	                    postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   924	                    return true
   925	                }
   926	                if (mockDragReturnInProgress) {
   927	                    mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   928	                    pressedMockField = null
   929	                    pressedRememberMe = false
   930	                    pressedBackChevron = false
   931	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   932	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   933	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   934	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   935	                    lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
   936	                    pressedMockDropdown = null
   937	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   938	                    pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   939	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   940	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   941	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   942	                    return true
   943	                }
   944	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   945	                    if (isMockFlowSwitchAnimating()) {
   946	                        pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   947	                        pressedSignInPrompt = false
   948	                        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   949	                        return true
   950	                    }
   951	                    val requestedFlow = when {
   952	                        settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y) -> {
   953	                            MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount
   954	                        }
   955	                        settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y) -> {
   956	                            MockAccountFlow.SignIn
   957	                        }
   958	                        settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y) -> {
   959	                            MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword
   960	                        }
   961	                        else -> null
   962	                    }
   963	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   964	                    pressedSignInPrompt = false
   965	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   966	                    if (requestedFlow != null) {
   967	                        super.performClick()
   968	                        switchMockAccountFlow(requestedFlow)
   969	                        return true
   970	                    }
   971	                    val releasedDropdownOption = mockDropdownOptionHit(event.x, event.y)
   972	                    val releasedDropdown = mockDropdownHit(event.x, event.y)
   973	                    val releasedField = mockSignInFieldHit(event.x, event.y)
   974	                    val releasedRememberMe = mockRememberMeHit(event.x, event.y)
   975	                    val releasedBackChevron = mockBackChevronHit(event.x, event.y)
   976	                    val releasedCreateAvatarNav = mockCreateAvatarNavHit(event.x, event.y)
   977	                    val releasedForgotRecoveryTarget = mockForgotRecoveryTargetHit(event.x, event.y)
   978	                    val releasedForgotPasswordSubmit = mockForgotPasswordSubmitHit(event.x, event.y)
   979	                    val wasTouchingDropdownOptions = activeMockDropdownScroll != null ||
   980	                        mockDropdownOptionsHit(mockTouchDownX, mockTouchDownY) != null
   981	                    val wasScrollingDropdown = mockDropdownScrollMoved
   982	                    val wasPressingRememberMe = pressedRememberMe
   983	                    val wasPressingBackChevron = pressedBackChevron
   984	                    val wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget != null
   985	                    val wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit = pressedForgotPasswordSubmit
   986	                    val wasDraggingCreateAvatar = activeMockCreateAvatarDrag
   987	                    val createAvatarDragMoved = mockCreateAvatarDragMoved
   988	                    val createAvatarDragDirection = mockCreateAvatarDragDirection()
   989	                    val createAvatarDragProgress = mockCreateAvatarDragProgress()
   990	                    val pressedDropdown = pressedMockDropdown
   991	                    val pressedOption = pressedMockDropdownOption
   992	                    val pressedCreateAvatarNav = pressedMockCreateAvatarNav
   993	                    val pressedForgotRecoverySelection = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget
   994	                    val shouldSelectDropdownOption = !wasScrollingDropdown &&
   995	                        pressedOption != null &&
   996	                        pressedOption == releasedDropdownOption
   997	                    val shouldSelectForgotRecoveryTarget = pressedForgotRecoverySelection != null &&
   998	                        pressedForgotRecoverySelection == releasedForgotRecoveryTarget
   999	                    val shouldToggleDropdown = pressedDropdown != null && pressedDropdown == releasedDropdown
  1000	                    val shouldToggleRememberMe = wasPressingRememberMe && releasedRememberMe
  1001	                    val shouldCloseMockSignIn = wasPressingBackChevron && releasedBackChevron
  1002	                    val shouldSubmitForgotPassword = wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit && releasedForgotPasswordSubmit
  1003	                    val shouldNavigateCreateAvatar = pressedCreateAvatarNav != null &&
  1004	                        !createAvatarDragMoved &&
  1005	                        pressedCreateAvatarNav == releasedCreateAvatarNav
  1006	                    val shouldSwipeCreateAvatar = wasDraggingCreateAvatar &&
  1007	                        createAvatarDragMoved &&
  1008	                        createAvatarDragDirection != null &&
  1009	                        createAvatarDragProgress >= MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_DRAG_COMMIT_PROGRESS
  1010	                    val shouldFocusField = releasedField != null &&
  1011	                        !wasTouchingDropdownOptions &&
  1012	                        !wasPressingRememberMe &&
  1013	                        !wasPressingBackChevron &&
  1014	                        !wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget &&
  1015	                        !wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit &&
  1016	                        !wasDraggingCreateAvatar &&
  1017	                        pressedDropdown == null &&
  1018	                        pressedOption == null &&
  1019	                        pressedCreateAvatarNav == null &&
  1020	                        (pressedMockField == null || pressedMockField == releasedField)
  1021	                    pressedMockField = null
  1022	                    pressedRememberMe = false
  1023	                    pressedBackChevron = false
  1024	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  1025	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
  1026	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
  1027	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
  1028	                    lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
  1029	                    pressedMockDropdown = null
  1030	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  1031	                    this.pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  1032	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  1033	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  1034	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  1035	                    super.performClick()
  1036	                    if (shouldCloseMockSignIn) {
  1037	                        closeMockSignInScreen()
  1038	                    } else if (shouldSwipeCreateAvatar && createAvatarDragDirection != null) {
  1039	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
  1040	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1041	                        startMockCreateAvatarCarousel(createAvatarDragDirection, createAvatarDragProgress)
  1042	                    } else if (shouldNavigateCreateAvatar && pressedCreateAvatarNav != null) {
  1043	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
  1044	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1045	                        startMockCreateAvatarCarousel(pressedCreateAvatarNav)
  1046	                    } else if (wasDraggingCreateAvatar) {
  1047	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1048	                    } else if (shouldSelectDropdownOption && pressedOption != null) {
  1049	                        setMockDropdownValue(pressedOption)
  1050	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
  1051	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  1052	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  1053	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1054	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1055	                    } else if (shouldToggleDropdown && pressedDropdown != null) {
  1056	                        expandedMockDropdown = if (expandedMockDropdown == pressedDropdown) null else pressedDropdown
  1057	                        expandedMockDropdown?.let { prepareMockDropdownScroll(it) }
  1058	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  1059	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  1060	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1061	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1062	                    } else if (shouldToggleRememberMe) {
  1063	                        rememberMeChecked = !rememberMeChecked
  1064	                        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  1065	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1066	                    } else if (shouldSelectForgotRecoveryTarget && pressedForgotRecoverySelection != null) {
  1067	                        setMockForgotRecoveryTarget(pressedForgotRecoverySelection)
  1068	                    } else if (shouldSubmitForgotPassword) {
  1069	                        startMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  1070	                    } else if (shouldFocusField && releasedField != null) {
  1071	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
  1072	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  1073	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  1074	                        focusMockSignInField(releasedField)
  1075	                    } else if (wasTouchingDropdownOptions || wasScrollingDropdown) {
  1076	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1077	                    } else if (!wasPressingRememberMe && !wasPressingBackChevron && !wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget) {
  1078	                        if (expandedMockDropdown != null && activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1079	                            expandedMockDropdown = null
  1080	                            restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  1081	                            restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  1082	                        }
  1083	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1084	                    } else {
  1085	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1086	                    }
  1087	                    return true
  1088	                }
  1089	                val tapStayedWithinSlop =
  1090	                    abs(event.x - mockTouchDownX) <= touchSlop &&
  1091	                        abs(event.y - mockTouchDownY) <= touchSlop
  1092	                val shouldOpenMockCreateAccount = tapStayedWithinSlop &&
  1093	                    settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
  1094	                val shouldOpenMockSignIn = tapStayedWithinSlop &&
  1095	                    settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
  1096	                val shouldOpenMockForgotPassword = tapStayedWithinSlop &&
  1097	                    settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
  1098	                val releasedAvatarProfile = settledAvatarHitProfile(event.x, event.y)
  1099	                val swipeDirection = portraitProfileSwipeDirection()
  1100	                val swipeProgress = portraitProfileDragProgress()
  1101	                val shouldSwipeProfile = isPortraitIntroLayout() &&
  1102	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null &&
  1103	                    portraitProfileSwipeMoved &&
  1104	                    swipeProgress >= PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SWIPE_COMMIT_PROGRESS &&
  1105	                    swipeDirection != null
  1106	                val shouldNavigate = tapStayedWithinSlop &&
  1107	                    !shouldSwipeProfile &&
  1108	                    !portraitProfileSwipeMoved &&
  1109	                    releasedAvatarProfile != null
  1110	                clickAvatarProfile = releasedAvatarProfile
  1111	                pressedAvatarProfile = null
  1112	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
  1113	                pressedSignInPrompt = false
  1114	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
  1115	                if (shouldSwipeProfile && swipeDirection != null) {
  1116	                    clickAvatarProfile = null
  1117	                    portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
  1118	                    portraitProfileDragDeltaX = 0f
  1119	                    startPortraitProfileCarousel(swipeDirection, swipeProgress)
  1120	                    true
  1121	                } else if (shouldOpenMockCreateAccount) {
  1122	                    super.performClick()
  1123	                    openMockCreateAccountScreen()
  1124	                    true
  1125	                } else if (shouldOpenMockSignIn) {
  1126	                    super.performClick()
  1127	                    openMockSignInScreen()
  1128	                    true
  1129	                } else if (shouldOpenMockForgotPassword) {
  1130	                    super.performClick()
  1131	                    openMockForgotPasswordScreen()
  1132	                    true
  1133	                } else if (shouldNavigate) {
  1134	                    performClick()
  1135	                    true
  1136	                } else {
  1137	                    clickAvatarProfile = null
  1138	                    portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
  1139	                    portraitProfileDragDeltaX = 0f
  1140	                    postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1141	                    true
  1142	                }
  1143	            }
  1144	            MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL -> {
  1145	                pressedAvatarProfile = null
  1146	                clickAvatarProfile = null
  1147	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
  1148	                pressedSignInPrompt = false
  1149	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
  1150	                pressedMockField = null
  1151	                pressedRememberMe = false
  1152	                pressedBackChevron = false
  1153	                pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  1154	                activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
  1155	                mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
  1156	                mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
  1157	                lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
  1158	                pressedMockDropdown = null
  1159	                pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  1160	                pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  1161	                pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  1162	                activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  1163	                mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  1164	                mockDragReturnInProgress = false
  1165	                mockDragReturnEligibleAtTouchDown = false
  1166	                portraitAuthScrollMoved = false
  1167	                portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
  1168	                portraitProfileDragDeltaX = 0f
  1169	                true
  1170	            }
  1171	            else -> pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
  1172	                pressedSignInPrompt ||
  1173	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
  1174	                pressedAvatarProfile != null ||
  1175	                mockSignInStartMillis != null
  1176	        }
  1177	    }

  1179	    override fun onCheckIsTextEditor(): Boolean {
  1180	        return mockSignInStartMillis != null && !isMockSignInClosing()
  1181	    }
  1182	
  1183	    override fun onCreateInputConnection(outAttrs: EditorInfo): InputConnection? {
  1184	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return null
  1185	        outAttrs.inputType = when (field) {
  1186	            MockSignInField.Password,
  1187	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_PASSWORD
  1188	            MockSignInField.Email -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_EMAIL_ADDRESS
  1189	            MockSignInField.Month,
  1190	            MockSignInField.Day,
  1191	            MockSignInField.Year -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_NUMBER
  1192	            else -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_NORMAL
  1193	        }
  1194	        outAttrs.imeOptions = if (field == activeMockFields().lastOrNull()) {
  1195	            EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE
  1196	        } else {
  1197	            EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_NEXT
  1198	        }
  1199	        val textLength = mockSignInFieldText(field).length
  1200	        outAttrs.initialSelStart = textLength
  1201	        outAttrs.initialSelEnd = textLength
  1202	        return MockSignInInputConnection()
  1203	    }
  1204	
  1205	    override fun onKeyDown(keyCode: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  1206	        if (focusedMockField != null) {
  1207	            return handleMockSignInKeyEvent(keyCode, event) || super.onKeyDown(keyCode, event)
  1208	        }
  1209	        return super.onKeyDown(keyCode, event)
  1210	    }
  1211	
  1212	    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
  1213	    override fun onKeyMultiple(keyCode: Int, repeatCount: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  1214	        val characters = event.getCharacters()
  1215	        if (focusedMockField != null && !characters.isNullOrEmpty()) {
  1216	            return appendMockSignInInput(characters)
  1217	        }
  1218	        return super.onKeyMultiple(keyCode, repeatCount, event)
  1219	    }
  1220	
  1221	    override fun performClick(): Boolean {
  1222	        super.performClick()
  1223	        if (!isFinalFrameSettled()) return false
  1224	        val profile = clickAvatarProfile ?: return false
  1225	        onAvatarSelected?.invoke(profile)
  1226	        clickAvatarProfile = null
  1227	        return true
  1228	    }

  1230	    private fun drawSettlingLogo(
  1231	        canvas: Canvas,
  1232	        stageLeft: Float,
  1233	        stageTop: Float,
  1234	        stageScale: Float,
  1235	        progress: Float,
  1236	        alpha: Float
  1237	    ) {
  1238	        val logoEntry = bouncySegmentMs(progress, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS, LOGO_ENTRY_END_MS)
  1239	        val finalProgress = easedSegmentMs(progress, FINAL_SETTLE_START_MS, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
  1240	        val focusShiftY = mockLogoFocusShiftY()
  1241	        val portraitAmount = portraitIntroAmount()
  1242	        val portraitLogo = Bounds(
  1243	            (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - 600f * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE) / 2f +
  1244	                PORTRAIT_LOGO_VISIBLE_CENTER_OFFSET_X,
  1245	            portraitLogoTop(),
  1246	            600f * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE,
  1247	            LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE
  1248	        )
  1249	        val portraitCenteredLogo = Bounds(
  1250	            x = portraitLogo.x + portraitLogo.w / 2f -
  1251	                portraitLogo.w * LOGO_PRE_SETTLE_SCALE / 2f,
  1252	            y = portraitStackCenter(0.5f) -
  1253	                portraitLogo.h * LOGO_PRE_SETTLE_SCALE / 2f,
  1254	            w = portraitLogo.w * LOGO_PRE_SETTLE_SCALE,
  1255	            h = portraitLogo.h * LOGO_PRE_SETTLE_SCALE
  1256	        )
  1257	        val base = offsetBounds(
  1258	            lerpBounds(Bounds(222f, 150f, 750f, 535f), portraitCenteredLogo, portraitAmount),
  1259	            focusShiftY
  1260	        )
  1261	        val final = offsetBounds(
  1262	            lerpBounds(Bounds(297f, LOGO_FINAL_TOP, 600f, LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT), portraitLogo, portraitAmount),
  1263	            focusShiftY
  1264	        )
  1265	
  1266	        if (logoEntry >= 0.999f) {
  1267	            drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoCombined, lerpBounds(base, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1268	            return
  1269	        }
  1270	
  1271	        val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(Bounds(447f, 1037f, 300f, 214f), base, logoEntry)
  1272	        val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(Bounds(453f, -417f, 300f, 214f), base, logoEntry)
  1273	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoSimple, lerpBounds(simpleEntry, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1274	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoEyes, lerpBounds(eyesEntry, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1275	    }
  1276	
  1277	    private fun drawPortraitProfile(
  1278	        canvas: Canvas,
  1279	        stage: StageMetrics,
  1280	        yOffset: Float,
  1281	        alpha: Float
  1282	    ) {
  1283	        val slideDistance = portraitAvatarSize()
  1284	        val dragDirection = portraitProfileSwipeDirection()
  1285	        val dragProgress = portraitProfileDragProgress()
  1286	        val carouselProgress = portraitProfileCarouselProgress()
  1287	        val carouselDirection = portraitProfileCarouselDirection.stageDirection
  1288	
  1289	        if (portraitProfileSwipeMoved && dragDirection != null && !isPortraitProfileCarouselAnimating()) {
  1290	            val direction = dragDirection.stageDirection
  1291	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1292	                canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha * (1f - dragProgress), portraitProfileIndex,
  1293	                -direction * dragProgress * slideDistance
  1294	            )
  1295	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1296	                canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha * dragProgress, portraitProfileIndexForDirection(dragDirection),
  1297	                direction * (1f - dragProgress) * slideDistance
  1298	            )
  1299	        } else if (isPortraitProfileCarouselAnimating()) {
  1300	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1301	                canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha * (1f - carouselProgress), portraitProfileCarouselFromIndex,
  1302	                -carouselDirection * carouselProgress * slideDistance
  1303	            )
  1304	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1305	                canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha * carouselProgress, portraitProfileCarouselToIndex,
  1306	                carouselDirection * (1f - carouselProgress) * slideDistance
  1307	            )
  1308	        } else {
  1309	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha, portraitProfileIndex, 0f)
  1310	        }
  1311	    }

  1313	    private fun drawWelcomeAvatarStack(
  1314	        canvas: Canvas,
  1315	        stage: StageMetrics,
  1316	        yOffset: Float,
  1317	        alpha: Float
  1318	    ) {
  1319	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  1320	            drawPortraitProfile(canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha)
  1321	            return
  1322	        }
  1323	
  1324	        val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY()
  1325	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, steveAvatar, Bounds(152f, 450f + stackShiftY + yOffset, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1326	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, martinAvatar, Bounds(390f, 450f + stackShiftY + yOffset, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1327	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, jannyAvatar, Bounds(628f, 454f + stackShiftY + yOffset, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1328	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, guestAvatar, Bounds(866f, 450f + stackShiftY + yOffset, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1329	
  1330	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, steveName, Bounds(130f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + yOffset, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1331	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, martinName, Bounds(368f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + yOffset, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1332	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, jannyName, Bounds(606f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + yOffset, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1333	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, guestName, Bounds(854f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + yOffset, 200f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1334	    }

  1336	
  1337	    private fun drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1338	        canvas: Canvas,
  1339	        stage: StageMetrics,
  1340	        yOffset: Float,
  1341	        alpha: Float,
  1342	        profileIndex: Int,
  1343	        offsetX: Float
  1344	    ) {
  1345	        val profile = FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS[profileIndex].profile
  1346	        val avatarBitmap = when (profile) {
  1347	            CinerificProfile.Steve -> steveAvatar
  1348	            CinerificProfile.Martin -> martinAvatar
  1349	            CinerificProfile.Janny -> jannyAvatar
  1350	            CinerificProfile.Guest -> guestAvatar
  1351	        }
  1352	        val nameBitmap = when (profile) {
  1353	            CinerificProfile.Steve -> steveName
  1354	            CinerificProfile.Martin -> martinName
  1355	            CinerificProfile.Janny -> jannyName
  1356	            CinerificProfile.Guest -> guestName
  1357	        }
  1358	        val avatarSize = portraitAvatarSize()
  1359	        val nameWidth = (if (profile == CinerificProfile.Guest) 200f else 220f) * PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SCALE
  1360	        drawFigmaBitmap(
  1361	            canvas,
  1362	            avatarBitmap,
  1363	            Bounds(
  1364	                (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - avatarSize) / 2f + offsetX,
  1365	                portraitAvatarTop() + yOffset,
  1366	                avatarSize,
  1367	                avatarSize
  1368	            ),
  1369	            stage.left,
  1370	            stage.top,
  1371	            stage.scale,
  1372	            alpha
  1373	        )
  1374	        drawFigmaBitmap(
  1375	            canvas,
  1376	            nameBitmap,
  1377	            Bounds(
  1378	                (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - nameWidth) / 2f + offsetX,
  1379	                portraitProfileNameTop() + yOffset,
  1380	                nameWidth,
  1381	                portraitProfileNameHeight()
  1382	            ),
  1383	            stage.left,
  1384	            stage.top,
  1385	            stage.scale,
  1386	            alpha
  1387	        )
  1388	    }
  1389	
  1390	    private fun drawIntroBackground(canvas: Canvas, solidProgress: Float, gradientProgress: Float) {
  1391	        val standardSignInBackground = standardSignInBackgroundForSize()
  1392	        if (gradientProgress <= 0.001f) {
  1393	            canvas.drawColor(lerpColor(COLOR_FRAME_1, COLOR_FRAME_2, solidProgress))
  1394	            return
  1395	        }
  1396	
  1397	        val solid = lerpColor(COLOR_FRAME_1, COLOR_FRAME_2, solidProgress)
  1398	        backgroundPaint.shader = LinearGradient(
  1399	            0f,
  1400	            0f,
  1401	            0f,
  1402	            height.toFloat(),
  1403	            intArrayOf(
  1404	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_TOP, gradientProgress),
  1405	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_CENTER, gradientProgress),
  1406	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_BOTTOM, gradientProgress)
  1407	            ),
  1408	            null,
  1409	            Shader.TileMode.CLAMP
  1410	        )
  1411	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), backgroundPaint)
  1412	        backgroundPaint.shader = null
  1413	
  1414	        if (standardSignInBackground != null) {
  1415	            drawStandardSignInBlackMatte(canvas, gradientProgress)
  1416	            drawStandardSignInBackground(canvas, standardSignInBackground, gradientProgress)
  1417	        }
  1418	    }
  1419	
  1420	    private fun drawStandardSignInBlackMatte(canvas: Canvas, alpha: Float) {
  1421	        backgroundPaint.color = COLOR_FRAME_1
  1422	        backgroundPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1423	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), backgroundPaint)
  1424	        backgroundPaint.alpha = 255
  1425	    }
  1426	
  1427	    private fun drawStandardSignInBackground(canvas: Canvas, bitmap: Bitmap, alpha: Float) {
  1428	        tempRect.set(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat())
  1429	        imagePaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * STANDARD_BACKGROUND_SETTLED_ALPHA * 255f).roundToInt()
  1430	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempRect, imagePaint)
  1431	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  1432	    }
  1433	
  1434	    private fun drawFigmaBitmap(
  1435	        canvas: Canvas,
  1436	        bitmap: Bitmap,
  1437	        bounds: Bounds,
  1438	        stageLeft: Float,
  1439	        stageTop: Float,
  1440	        stageScale: Float,
  1441	        alpha: Float
  1442	    ) {
  1443	        tempRect.set(
  1444	            stageLeft + bounds.x * stageScale,
  1445	            stageTop + bounds.y * stageScale,
  1446	            stageLeft + (bounds.x + bounds.w) * stageScale,
  1447	            stageTop + (bounds.y + bounds.h) * stageScale
  1448	        )
  1449	        imagePaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1450	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempRect, imagePaint)
  1451	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  1452	    }
  1453	
  1454	    private fun drawAccountPrompt(
  1455	        canvas: Canvas,
  1456	        stageLeft: Float,
  1457	        stageTop: Float,
  1458	        stageScale: Float,
  1459	        yOffset: Float,
  1460	        alpha: Float
  1461	    ) {
  1462	        if (usesPortraitStackedAuthLayout(activeMockFlow) ||
  1463	            usesPortraitStackedAuthLayout(outgoingMockFlow)
  1464	        ) {
  1465	            return
  1466	        }
  1467	        val isLandscape = width > height
  1468	        val portraitAmount = portraitIntroAmount()
  1469	        val textSize = lerpFloat(
  1470	            signInAccountPromptTextSize(),
  1471	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE * PORTRAIT_ACTION_SCALE,
  1472	            portraitAmount
  1473	        ) * stageScale
  1474	        val paintAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1475	        accountPromptPaint.textSize = textSize
  1476	        formTitlePaint.textSize = textSize
  1477	        accountPromptPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  1478	        formTitlePaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  1479	
  1480	        val accountPromptShiftY = if (isLandscape) SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_ACCOUNT_PROMPT_EXTRA_SHIFT_Y else 0f
  1481	        val standardCenterY = stageTop +
  1482	            (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + accountPromptShiftY + yOffset) * stageScale
  1483	        val secondaryMetrics = accountPromptPaint.fontMetrics
  1484	        val primaryMetrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  1485	        fun promptCenterY(portraitCenterY: Float): Float = lerpFloat(
  1486	            standardCenterY,
  1487	            stageTop + (portraitCenterY + yOffset) * stageScale,
  1488	            portraitAmount
  1489	        )
  1490	        val createCenterY = promptCenterY(portraitPromptCreateCenterY())
  1491	        val signInCenterY = promptCenterY(portraitPromptSignInCenterY())
  1492	        val forgotCenterY = promptCenterY(portraitPromptForgotCenterY())
  1493	        val createBaselineY = createCenterY - (secondaryMetrics.ascent + secondaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1494	        val signInBaselineY = signInCenterY - (primaryMetrics.ascent + primaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1495	        val forgotBaselineY = forgotCenterY - (secondaryMetrics.ascent + secondaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1496	        val portraitCenterX = FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH / 2f
  1497	        if (
  1498	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount &&
  1499	            outgoingMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount
  1500	        ) {
  1501	            accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1502	            canvas.drawText(
  1503	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_TEXT,
  1504	                stageLeft + lerpFloat(accountPromptCreateCenterX(), portraitCenterX, portraitAmount) * stageScale,
  1505	                createBaselineY,
  1506	                accountPromptPaint
  1507	            )
  1508	        }
  1509	        if (
  1510	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.SignIn &&
  1511	            outgoingMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.SignIn
  1512	        ) {
  1513	            formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1514	            canvas.drawText(
  1515	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
  1516	                stageLeft + lerpFloat(ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X, portraitCenterX, portraitAmount) * stageScale,
  1517	                signInBaselineY,
  1518	                formTitlePaint
  1519	            )
  1520	        }
  1521	        if (
  1522	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword &&
  1523	            outgoingMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword
  1524	        ) {
  1525	            accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1526	            canvas.drawText(
  1527	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_FORGOT_TEXT,
  1528	                stageLeft + lerpFloat(accountPromptForgotCenterX(), portraitCenterX, portraitAmount) * stageScale,
  1529	                forgotBaselineY,
  1530	                accountPromptPaint
  1531	            )
  1532	        }
  1533	        accountPromptPaint.alpha = 255
  1534	        accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1535	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1536	    }

  1538	    private fun drawMockSignInForm(
  1539	        canvas: Canvas,
  1540	        stageLeft: Float,
  1541	        stageTop: Float,
  1542	        stageScale: Float,
  1543	        progress: Float,
  1544	        formFocusMotion: Float,
  1545	        updateLabelAnimation: Boolean = true
  1546	    ) {
  1547	        if (updateLabelAnimation) {
  1548	            updateMockFormLabelAnimation()
  1549	        }
  1550	
  1551	        val alpha = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1552	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1553	            drawMockCreateAccountForm(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha, formFocusMotion)
  1554	            return
  1555	        }
  1556	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) {
  1557	            drawMockForgotPasswordForm(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha, formFocusMotion)
  1558	            return
  1559	        }
  1560	
  1561	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - alpha)
  1562	        val fieldFillAlpha = (alpha * 26f).roundToInt()
  1563	        val fieldStrokeAlpha = (alpha * 185f).roundToInt()
  1564	        val baseStage = StageMetrics(stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale)
  1565	        val usernameY = mockPrimaryFormY() + yOffset
  1566	        val passwordY = usernameY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + mockFormFieldGap()
  1567	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)
  1568	        val contentStage = mockSignInContentStageMetrics(baseStage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  1569	        val contentLeft = contentStage.left
  1570	        val contentTop = contentStage.top
  1571	        val contentScale = contentStage.scale
  1572	        val fieldX = mockFormX()
  1573	
  1574	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fieldFillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1575	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(fieldStrokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1576	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * contentScale
  1577	        formCaretPaint.strokeWidth = 1.45f * contentScale
  1578	
  1579	        val rememberMeY = passwordY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TOP_GAP + focusShiftY
  1580	        val titleFieldY = usernameY + if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) 0f else focusShiftY
  1581	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  1582	            canvas.save()
  1583	            canvas.clipRect(
  1584	                0f,
  1585	                stageTop + portraitLogoVisibleBottom() * stageScale,
  1586	                width.toFloat(),
  1587	                portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(baseStage)
  1588	            )
  1589	        }
  1590	        drawMockSignInTitle(canvas, titleFieldY, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1591	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  1592	            canvas.restore()
  1593	            canvas.save()
  1594	            canvas.clipRect(
  1595	                0f,
  1596	                portraitAuthViewportTopPx(baseStage),
  1597	                width.toFloat(),
  1598	                portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(baseStage)
  1599	            )
  1600	        }
  1601	        drawMockSignInField(canvas, MockSignInField.Username, fieldX, usernameY + focusShiftY, contentLeft, contentTop, contentScale, alpha)
  1602	        drawMockSignInField(canvas, MockSignInField.Password, fieldX, passwordY + focusShiftY, contentLeft, contentTop, contentScale, alpha)
  1603	        drawRememberMeCheckbox(
  1604	            canvas,
  1605	            fieldX,
  1606	            rememberMeY,
  1607	            contentLeft,
  1608	            contentTop,
  1609	            contentScale,
  1610	            alpha
  1611	        )
  1612	        drawMockInputDimOverlayAndActiveControl(
  1613	            canvas,
  1614	            contentLeft,
  1615	            contentTop,
  1616	            contentScale,
  1617	            alpha,
  1618	            yOffset,
  1619	            focusShiftY
  1620	        )
  1621	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  1622	            canvas.restore()
  1623	        }
  1624	
  1625	        formLabelPaint.alpha = 255
  1626	        formInputPaint.alpha = 255
  1627	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1628	        formCaretPaint.alpha = 255
  1629	    }

  1886	
  1887	    private fun drawMockInputDimOverlayAndActiveControl(
  1888	        canvas: Canvas,
  1889	        stageLeft: Float,
  1890	        stageTop: Float,
  1891	        stageScale: Float,
  1892	        alpha: Float,
  1893	        yOffset: Float,
  1894	        focusShiftY: Float
  1895	    ) {
  1896	        val dimProgress = mockInputDimProgress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1897	        if (dimProgress <= 0.01f) return
  1898	
  1899	        inputDimPaint.alpha = (dimProgress * MOCK_INPUT_DIM_ALPHA * 255f).roundToInt()
  1900	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), inputDimPaint)
  1901	        inputDimPaint.alpha = 255
  1902	
  1903	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 26f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1904	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 185f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1905	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1906	        formCaretPaint.strokeWidth = 1.45f * stageScale
  1907	
  1908	        val activeDropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: mockInputDimActiveDropdown
  1909	        if (activeDropdown != null && activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1910	            drawMockDropdownControl(
  1911	                canvas,
  1912	                activeDropdown,
  1913	                mockDropdownX(activeDropdown),
  1914	                mockDropdownY(activeDropdown, yOffset, focusShiftY),
  1915	                stageLeft,
  1916	                stageTop,
  1917	                stageScale,
  1918	                alpha,
  1919	                mockDropdownWidth(activeDropdown)
  1920	            )
  1921	            if (expandedMockDropdown == activeDropdown) {
  1922	                drawMockDropdownOptions(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1923	            }
  1924	            return
  1925	        }
  1926	
  1927	        val activeField = focusedMockField ?: mockInputDimActiveField ?: return
  1928	        drawMockSignInField(
  1929	            canvas,
  1930	            activeField,
  1931	            mockActiveFieldX(activeField),
  1932	            mockActiveFieldY(activeField, yOffset, focusShiftY),
  1933	            stageLeft,
  1934	            stageTop,
  1935	            stageScale,
  1936	            alpha,
  1937	            mockActiveFieldWidth(activeField)
  1938	        )
  1939	    }

  1969	        canvas: Canvas,
  1970	        fieldTopY: Float,
  1971	        stageLeft: Float,
  1972	        stageTop: Float,
  1973	        stageScale: Float,
  1974	        alpha: Float
  1975	    ) {
  1976	        val motion = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1977	        val portrait = isPortraitIntroLayout()
  1978	        val portraitTextSize = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE * PORTRAIT_ACTION_SCALE * stageScale
  1979	        val sourceTextSize = if (portrait) portraitTextSize else signInAccountPromptTextSize() * stageScale
  1980	        val targetTextSize = if (portrait) portraitTextSize else MOCK_FORM_TITLE_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  1981	        val sourceCenterX = stageLeft + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X * stageScale
  1982	        val targetCenterX = stageLeft + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X * stageScale
  1983	        val sourceCenterY = stageTop + (if (portrait) {
  1984	            portraitPromptSignInCenterY()
  1985	        } else {
  1986	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY()
  1987	        }) * stageScale
  1988	        formTitlePaint.textSize = sourceTextSize
  1989	        val sourceMetrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  1990	        val sourceBaselineY = sourceCenterY - (sourceMetrics.ascent + sourceMetrics.descent) / 2f

  1994	            stageTop,
  1995	            stageScale,

  1997	        )

  2002	        canvas.drawText(
  2003	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,

  2005	            lerpFloat(sourceBaselineY, targetBaselineY, motion),
  2006	            formTitlePaint
  2007	        )
  2008	    }

  2102	    private fun mockFormTitleTargetBaseline(
  2103	        title: String,
  2104	        contentTopY: Float,
  2105	        stageTop: Float,
  2106	        stageScale: Float,
  2107	        targetTextSize: Float
  2108	    ): Float {
  2109	        formTitlePaint.textSize = targetTextSize
  2110	        val titlePath = Path()
  2111	        val titleBounds = RectF()
  2112	        formTitlePaint.getTextPath(title, 0, title.length, 0f, 0f, titlePath)
  2113	        titlePath.computeBounds(titleBounds, true)
  2114	        val contentScaleRatio = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) portraitCreateContentScale() else 1f
  2115	        return stageTop + contentTopY * stageScale -
  2116	            mockFormFieldGap() * 2f * stageScale * contentScaleRatio -
  2117	            titleBounds.bottom +
  2118	            MOCK_FORM_TITLE_OPTICAL_BOTTOM_OFFSET * stageScale * contentScaleRatio
  2119	    }

  2121	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordButton(
  2122	        canvas: Canvas,
  2123	        x: Float,
  2124	        y: Float,
  2125	        stageLeft: Float,
  2126	        stageTop: Float,
  2127	        stageScale: Float,
  2128	        alpha: Float,
  2129	        buttonWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  2130	    ) {
  2131	        tempRect.set(
  2132	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  2133	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  2134	            stageLeft + (x + buttonWidth) * stageScale,
  2135	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  2136	        )
  2137	        val radius = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_RADIUS * stageScale
  2138	        val submissionState = forgotPasswordSubmissionState
  2139	        val enabled = mockForgotPasswordCanSubmit()
  2140	        val pressed = pressedForgotPasswordSubmit && enabled
  2141	        val fillAlpha = when {
  2142	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 86f
  2143	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 72f
  2144	            pressed -> 92f
  2145	            enabled -> 68f
  2146	            else -> 18f
  2147	        }
  2148	        val strokeAlpha = when {
  2149	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 245f
  2150	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 228f
  2151	            pressed -> 238f
  2152	            enabled -> 210f
  2153	            else -> 118f
  2154	        }
  2155	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = null
  2156	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * fillAlpha).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  2157	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * strokeAlpha).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  2158	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2159	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2160	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2161	        if (submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading) {
  2162	            drawMockForgotPasswordButtonShimmer(canvas, tempRect, radius, alpha)
  2163	        }
  2164	
  2165	        formTitlePaint.textSize = MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2166	        formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  2167	        val metrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  2168	        val baselineY = tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  2169	        val maxTextWidth = buttonWidth * stageScale - MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale * 2f
  2170	        val showCheck = submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent
  2171	        val checkSize = if (showCheck) MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SENT_CHECK_SIZE * stageScale else 0f
  2172	        val checkGap = if (showCheck) MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SENT_CHECK_GAP * stageScale else 0f
  2173	        val textMaxWidth = (maxTextWidth - checkSize - checkGap).coerceAtLeast(maxTextWidth * 0.5f)
  2174	        val buttonText = trailingFittingText(
  2175	            mockForgotPasswordButtonText(),
  2176	            if (showCheck) textMaxWidth else maxTextWidth,
  2177	            formTitlePaint
  2178	        )
  2179	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * when {
  2180	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 226f
  2181	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 255f
  2182	            enabled -> 255f
  2183	            else -> 138f
  2184	        }).roundToInt()
  2185	        formTitlePaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2186	        val textX = if (showCheck) {
  2187	            val textWidth = formTitlePaint.measureText(buttonText)
  2188	            val groupWidth = checkSize + checkGap + textWidth
  2189	            val groupLeft = tempRect.centerX() - groupWidth / 2f
  2190	            val checkLeft = groupLeft
  2191	            val checkCenterY = tempRect.centerY()
  2192	            backChevronPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2193	            backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = 2.1f * stageScale
  2194	            canvas.drawLine(
  2195	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.18f,
  2196	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.03f,
  2197	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.42f,
  2198	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.26f,
  2199	                backChevronPaint
  2200	            )
  2201	            canvas.drawLine(
  2202	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.42f,
  2203	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.26f,
  2204	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.84f,
  2205	                checkCenterY - checkSize * 0.28f,
  2206	                backChevronPaint
  2207	            )
  2208	            groupLeft + checkSize + checkGap + textWidth / 2f
  2209	        } else {
  2210	            tempRect.centerX()
  2211	        }
  2212	        canvas.drawText(
  2213	            buttonText,
  2214	            textX,
  2215	            baselineY,
  2216	            formTitlePaint
  2217	        )
  2218	    }
  2219	
  2220	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordButtonShimmer(
  2221	        canvas: Canvas,
  2222	        buttonRect: RectF,
  2223	        radius: Float,
  2224	        alpha: Float
  2225	    ) {
  2226	        val progress = mockForgotPasswordSubmissionProgress()
  2227	        val sweepWidth = buttonRect.width() * 0.44f
  2228	        val sweepCenterX = buttonRect.left + buttonRect.width() * (progress * 1.35f - 0.18f)
  2229	        val sweepLeft = sweepCenterX - sweepWidth / 2f
  2230	        val sweepRight = sweepCenterX + sweepWidth / 2f
  2231	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = LinearGradient(
  2232	            sweepLeft,
  2233	            buttonRect.top,
  2234	            sweepRight,
  2235	            buttonRect.top,
  2236	            intArrayOf(
  2237	                Color.argb(0, 255, 255, 255),
  2238	                Color.argb((alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 54f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255),
  2239	                Color.argb(0, 255, 255, 255)
  2240	            ),
  2241	            floatArrayOf(0f, 0.5f, 1f),
  2242	            Shader.TileMode.CLAMP
  2243	        )
  2244	        canvas.save()
  2245	        tempPath.reset()
  2246	        tempPath.addRoundRect(buttonRect, radius, radius, Path.Direction.CW)
  2247	        canvas.clipPath(tempPath)
  2248	        canvas.drawRect(sweepLeft, buttonRect.top, sweepRight, buttonRect.bottom, formFieldFillPaint)
  2249	        canvas.restore()
  2250	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = null
  2251	    }

  2253	    private fun drawMockForgotRecoverySelector(
  2254	        canvas: Canvas,
  2255	        x: Float,
  2256	        y: Float,

  2258	        stageTop: Float,

  2279	        )

  2297	            alpha
  2298	        )

  2300	            canvas,
  2301	            MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Password,
  2302	            layout.passwordBoxX,

  2304	            layout.boxWidth,
  2305	            stageScale,

  2310	    private fun drawMockForgotRecoveryOption(
  2311	        canvas: Canvas,
  2312	        target: MockForgotRecoveryTarget,
  2313	        left: Float,
  2314	        top: Float,
  2315	        boxWidth: Float,
  2316	        stageScale: Float,
  2317	        alpha: Float
  2318	    ) {
  2319	        val right = left + boxWidth
  2320	        val bottom = top + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_BOX_HEIGHT * stageScale
  2321	        tempRect.set(left, top, right, bottom)
  2322	        val selected = forgotRecoveryTarget == target
  2323	        val pressed = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == target
  2324	        val fillAlpha = (alpha * when {
  2325	            pressed -> 82f
  2326	            selected -> 64f
  2327	            else -> 14f
  2328	        }).roundToInt()
  2329	        val strokeAlpha = (alpha * when {
  2330	            pressed -> 245f
  2331	            selected -> 235f
  2332	            else -> 142f
  2333	        }).roundToInt()
  2334	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  2335	
  2336	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  2337	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(strokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  2338	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2339	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2340	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2341	
  2342	        formInputPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_OPTION_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2343	        formInputPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * if (selected || pressed) 255f else 178f).roundToInt()
  2344	        formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  2345	        val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  2346	        canvas.drawText(
  2347	            target.displayText,
  2348	            tempRect.centerX(),
  2349	            tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f,
  2350	            formInputPaint
  2351	        )
  2352	    }

  2354	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordBodyParagraph(
  2355	        canvas: Canvas,
  2356	        text: String,
  2357	        centerX: Float,
  2358	        topY: Float,
  2359	        maxWidth: Float,
  2360	        stageLeft: Float,
  2361	        stageTop: Float,
  2362	        stageScale: Float,
  2363	        alpha: Float
  2364	    ): Float {
  2365	        val paragraphs = text.split(Regex("\\n\\s*\\n"))
  2366	        val leadBottomY = drawMockFormParagraph(
  2367	            canvas,
  2368	            paragraphs.firstOrNull().orEmpty(),
  2369	            centerX,
  2370	            topY,
  2371	            maxWidth,
  2372	            stageLeft,
  2373	            stageTop,
  2374	            stageScale,
  2375	            alpha,
  2376	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_TEXT_SIZE,
  2377	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2378	            forceLineBreaks = true
  2379	        )
  2380	        val middleText = paragraphs.getOrNull(1) ?: return leadBottomY
  2381	        val middleBottomY = drawMockFormParagraph(
  2382	            canvas,
  2383	            middleText,
  2384	            centerX,
  2385	            leadBottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2386	            maxWidth,
  2387	            stageLeft,
  2388	            stageTop,
  2389	            stageScale,
  2390	            alpha,
  2391	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  2392	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2393	            Paint.Align.LEFT
  2394	        )
  2395	        val bottomText = paragraphs.drop(2).joinToString("\n\n")
  2396	        if (bottomText.isEmpty()) return middleBottomY
  2397	        return drawMockFormParagraph(
  2398	            canvas,
  2399	            bottomText,
  2400	            centerX,
  2401	            middleBottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2402	            maxWidth,
  2403	            stageLeft,
  2404	            stageTop,
  2405	            stageScale,
  2406	            alpha,
  2407	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  2408	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2409	            Paint.Align.LEFT
  2410	        )
  2411	    }
  2412	
  2413	    private fun drawMockFormParagraph(
  2414	        canvas: Canvas,
  2415	        text: String,
  2416	        centerX: Float,
  2417	        topY: Float,
  2418	        maxWidth: Float,
  2419	        stageLeft: Float,
  2420	        stageTop: Float,
  2421	        stageScale: Float,
  2422	        alpha: Float,
  2423	        textSize: Float,
  2424	        lineHeight: Float,
  2425	        textAlign: Paint.Align = Paint.Align.CENTER,
  2426	        forceLineBreaks: Boolean = false
  2427	    ): Float {
  2428	        formInputPaint.textSize = textSize * stageScale
  2429	        formInputPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 218f).roundToInt()
  2430	        formInputPaint.textAlign = textAlign
  2431	
  2432	        val lines = if (forceLineBreaks) {
  2433	            text.split('\n').flatMap { line ->
  2434	                wrappedTextLines(line, maxWidth * stageScale, formInputPaint)
  2435	            }
  2436	        } else {
  2437	            wrappedTextLines(text, maxWidth * stageScale, formInputPaint)
  2438	        }
  2439	        val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  2440	        val x = when (textAlign) {
  2441	            Paint.Align.LEFT -> stageLeft + (centerX - maxWidth / 2f) * stageScale
  2442	            Paint.Align.RIGHT -> stageLeft + (centerX + maxWidth / 2f) * stageScale
  2443	            Paint.Align.CENTER -> stageLeft + centerX * stageScale
  2444	        }
  2445	        var baselineY = stageTop + topY * stageScale - metrics.ascent
  2446	        lines.forEach { line ->
  2447	            canvas.drawText(line, x, baselineY, formInputPaint)
  2448	            baselineY += lineHeight * stageScale
  2449	        }
  2450	        return topY + lines.size * lineHeight
  2451	    }

  2453	    private fun drawMockCreateAccountAvatarText(
  2454	        canvas: Canvas,
  2455	        columnX: Float,
  2456	        firstFieldY: Float,
  2457	        stageLeft: Float,
  2458	        stageTop: Float,
  2459	        stageScale: Float,
  2460	        alpha: Float
  2461	    ) {
  2462	        val centerX = stageLeft + (columnX + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f) * stageScale
  2463	        val avatarBounds = mockCreateAvatarBounds(columnX, firstFieldY)
  2464	        val usesSeparatedMotionLayers = isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating() ||
  2465	            (activeMockCreateAvatarDrag && mockCreateAvatarDragMoved)
  2466	        if (usesSeparatedMotionLayers) {
  2467	            canvas.save()
  2468	            clipMockCreateAvatarCarousel(canvas, avatarBounds, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale)
  2469	            drawMockCreateAvatarMotionLayer(
  2470	                canvas = canvas,
  2471	                bitmaps = createAvatarBitmaps,
  2472	                bounds = avatarBounds,
  2473	                stageLeft = stageLeft,
  2474	                stageTop = stageTop,
  2475	                stageScale = stageScale,
  2476	                alpha = alpha
  2477	            )
  2478	            canvas.restore()
  2479	        } else {
  2480	            drawFigmaBitmap(
  2481	                canvas,
  2482	                createAvatarBitmaps[mockCreateAvatarIndex.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)],
  2483	                avatarBounds,
  2484	                stageLeft,
  2485	                stageTop,
  2486	                stageScale,
  2487	                alpha
  2488	            )
  2489	        }
  2490	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2491	            canvas,
  2492	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous,
  2493	            avatarBounds,
  2494	            stageLeft,
  2495	            stageTop,
  2496	            stageScale,
  2497	            alpha
  2498	        )
  2499	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2500	            canvas,
  2501	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next,
  2502	            avatarBounds,
  2503	            stageLeft,
  2504	            stageTop,
  2505	            stageScale,
  2506	            alpha
  2507	        )
  2508	        val usernameBaselineY = stageTop + mockCreateUsernameBaselineY(firstFieldY) * stageScale
  2509	        drawMockCreateUsername(canvas, centerX, usernameBaselineY, stageScale, alpha)
  2510	    }
  2511	
  2512	    private fun drawMockCreateAvatarTransitionOverlay(
  2513	        canvas: Canvas,
  2514	        stage: StageMetrics,
  2515	        mockMotion: Float,
  2516	        formFocusMotion: Float
  2517	    ) {
  2518	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) return
  2519	
  2520	        if (isMockCreateWelcomeTransition()) {
  2521	            drawMockCreateWelcomeTransition(
  2522	                canvas,
  2523	                stage,
  2524	                mockMotion.coerceIn(0f, 1f),
  2525	                formFocusMotion
  2526	            )
  2527	            return
  2528	        }
  2529	
  2530	        val outgoingFlow = outgoingMockFlow
  2531	        val transitionProgress = when {
  2532	            outgoingFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount -> {
  2533	                1f - FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFlowSwitchProgress())
  2534	            }
  2535	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount && outgoingFlow != null -> {
  2536	                FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFlowSwitchProgress())
  2537	            }
  2538	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount && isMockSignInAnimating() -> mockMotion
  2539	            else -> return
  2540	        }.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  2541	
  2542	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - transitionProgress)
  2543	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)
  2544	        val contentStage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  2545	
  2546	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  2547	            canvas.save()
  2548	            canvas.clipRect(
  2549	                0f,
  2550	                portraitAuthViewportTopPx(stage),
  2551	                width.toFloat(),
  2552	                portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(stage)
  2553	            )
  2554	        }
  2555	        drawMockCreateAccountAvatarText(
  2556	            canvas,
  2557	            mockCreateAvatarColumnX(),
  2558	            mockCreateAvatarSectionY(yOffset, focusShiftY),
  2559	            contentStage.left,
  2560	            contentStage.top,
  2561	            contentStage.scale,
  2562	            1f
  2563	        )
  2564	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  2565	            canvas.restore()
  2566	        }
  2567	    }
  2568	
  2569	    private fun isMockCreateWelcomeTransition(): Boolean {
  2570	        return isPortraitIntroLayout() &&
  2571	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount &&
  2572	            outgoingMockFlow == null &&
  2573	            isMockSignInAnimating()
  2574	    }
