  2576	    private fun drawMockCreateWelcomeTransition(
  2577	        canvas: Canvas,
  2578	        stage: StageMetrics,
  2579	        motion: Float,
  2580	        formFocusMotion: Float
  2581	    ) {
  2582	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)
  2583	        val accessoryYOffset = MOCK_SIGN_IN_STACK_EXIT_Y * (1f - motion)
  2584	        val contentStage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(stage, accessoryYOffset, focusShiftY)
  2585	        val columnX = mockCreateAvatarColumnX()
  2586	        val sectionY = mockCreateAvatarSectionY(accessoryYOffset, focusShiftY)
  2587	        val settledSectionY = mockCreateAvatarSectionY(0f, 0f)
  2588	        val settledContentStage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(stage, 0f, 0f)
  2589	        val settledAvatarBounds = mockCreateAvatarBounds(columnX, settledSectionY)
  2590	
  2591	        drawWelcomeAvatarRelatedAssetsForCreateTransition(canvas, stage, motion)
  2592	
  2593	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  2594	            canvas.save()
  2595	            canvas.clipRect(
  2596	                0f,
  2597	                portraitAuthViewportTopPx(stage),
  2598	                width.toFloat(),
  2599	                portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(stage)
  2600	            )
  2601	        }
  2602	        drawMockCreateUsername(
  2603	            canvas,
  2604	            contentStage.left + (columnX + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f) * contentStage.scale,
  2605	            contentStage.top + mockCreateUsernameBaselineY(sectionY) * contentStage.scale,
  2606	            contentStage.scale,
  2607	            1f
  2608	        )
  2609	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2610	            canvas,
  2611	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous,
  2612	            settledAvatarBounds,
  2613	            settledContentStage.left,
  2614	            settledContentStage.top,
  2615	            settledContentStage.scale,
  2616	            motion,
  2617	            mockCreateTransitionChevronCenterX(
  2618	                MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous,
  2619	                settledAvatarBounds,
  2620	                settledContentStage,
  2621	                motion
  2622	            )
  2623	        )
  2624	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2625	            canvas,
  2626	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next,
  2627	            settledAvatarBounds,
  2628	            settledContentStage.left,
  2629	            settledContentStage.top,
  2630	            settledContentStage.scale,
  2631	            motion,
  2632	            mockCreateTransitionChevronCenterX(
  2633	                MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next,
  2634	                settledAvatarBounds,
  2635	                settledContentStage,
  2636	                motion
  2637	            )
  2638	        )
  2639	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  2640	            canvas.restore()
  2641	        }
  2642	
  2643	        val destinationBounds = Bounds(
  2644	            (settledContentStage.left + settledAvatarBounds.x * settledContentStage.scale - stage.left) /
  2645	                stage.scale,
  2646	            (settledContentStage.top + settledAvatarBounds.y * settledContentStage.scale - stage.top) /
  2647	                stage.scale,
  2648	            settledAvatarBounds.w * settledContentStage.scale / stage.scale,
  2649	            settledAvatarBounds.h * settledContentStage.scale / stage.scale
  2650	        )
  2651	        val sourceBounds = mockCreateWelcomeAvatarSourceBounds()
  2652	        val movingBounds = lerpBounds(sourceBounds, destinationBounds, motion)
  2653	        val avatarIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)
  2654	
  2655	        drawFigmaBitmap(
  2656	            canvas,
  2657	            createAvatarBitmaps[avatarIndex],
  2658	            movingBounds,
  2659	            stage.left,
  2660	            stage.top,
  2661	            stage.scale,
  2662	            1f
  2663	        )
  2664	    }
  2665	
  2666	    private fun mockCreateTransitionChevronCenterX(
  2667	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  2668	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  2669	        contentStage: StageMetrics,
  2670	        motion: Float
  2671	    ): Float {
  2672	        val settledCenterX = contentStage.left +
  2673	            mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterX(direction, avatarBounds) * contentStage.scale
  2674	        val halfChevronWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_WIDTH * contentStage.scale / 2f
  2675	        val edgeCenterX = if (direction == MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous) {
  2676	            -halfChevronWidth
  2677	        } else {
  2678	            width.toFloat() + halfChevronWidth
  2679	        }
  2680	        return lerpFloat(edgeCenterX, settledCenterX, motion.coerceIn(0f, 1f))
  2681	    }
  2682	
  2683	    private fun mockCreateWelcomeAvatarSourceBounds(): Bounds {
  2684	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  2685	            val avatarSize = portraitAvatarSize()
  2686	            return Bounds(
  2687	                (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - avatarSize) / 2f,
  2688	                portraitAvatarTop(),
  2689	                avatarSize,
  2690	                avatarSize
  2691	            )
  2692	        }
  2693	
  2694	        val source = FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS[
  2695	            mockCreateAvatarIndex.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)
  2696	        ].bounds
  2697	        return Bounds(source.x, source.y + signInStackShiftY(), source.w, source.h)
  2698	    }
  2699	
  2700	    private fun drawWelcomeAvatarRelatedAssetsForCreateTransition(
  2701	        canvas: Canvas,
  2702	        stage: StageMetrics,
  2703	        motion: Float
  2704	    ) {
  2705	        val exitY = mockCreateRelatedAssetsExitY() * motion
  2706	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  2707	            val profile = FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS[portraitProfileIndex].profile
  2708	            val nameBitmap = when (profile) {
  2709	                CinerificProfile.Steve -> steveName
  2710	                CinerificProfile.Martin -> martinName
  2711	                CinerificProfile.Janny -> jannyName
  2712	                CinerificProfile.Guest -> guestName
  2713	            }
  2714	            val nameWidth = (if (profile == CinerificProfile.Guest) 200f else 220f) *
  2715	                PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SCALE
  2716	            drawFigmaBitmap(
  2717	                canvas,
  2718	                nameBitmap,
  2719	                Bounds(
  2720	                    (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - nameWidth) / 2f,
  2721	                    portraitProfileNameTop() + exitY,
  2722	                    nameWidth,
  2723	                    portraitProfileNameHeight()
  2724	                ),
  2725	                stage.left,
  2726	                stage.top,
  2727	                stage.scale,
  2728	                1f
  2729	            )
  2730	            return
  2731	        }
  2732	
  2733	        val selectedIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)
  2734	        val avatarBitmaps = listOf(steveAvatar, martinAvatar, jannyAvatar, guestAvatar)
  2735	        val nameBitmaps = listOf(steveName, martinName, jannyName, guestName)
  2736	        val nameWidths = listOf(220f, 220f, 220f, 200f)
  2737	        val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY()
  2738	        FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.forEachIndexed { index, target ->
  2739	            if (index != selectedIndex) {
  2740	                drawFigmaBitmap(
  2741	                    canvas,
  2742	                    avatarBitmaps[index],
  2743	                    Bounds(
  2744	                        target.bounds.x,
  2745	                        target.bounds.y + stackShiftY + exitY,
  2746	                        target.bounds.w,
  2747	                        target.bounds.h
  2748	                    ),
  2749	                    stage.left,
  2750	                    stage.top,
  2751	                    stage.scale,
  2752	                    1f
  2753	                )
  2754	            }
  2755	            drawFigmaBitmap(
  2756	                canvas,
  2757	                nameBitmaps[index],
  2758	                Bounds(
  2759	                    target.bounds.x - if (index == 3) 12f else 22f,
  2760	                    SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + exitY,
  2761	                    nameWidths[index],
  2762	                    72f
  2763	                ),
  2764	                stage.left,
  2765	                stage.top,
  2766	                stage.scale,
  2767	                1f
  2768	            )
  2769	        }
  2770	    }
  2771	
  2772	    private fun mockCreateRelatedAssetsExitY(): Float {
  2773	        return if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  2774	            portraitSafeBottom() - portraitProfileNameTop() + portraitProfileNameHeight()
  2775	        } else {
  2776	            FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT - (SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + signInStackShiftY()) + 72f
  2777	        }
  2778	    }
  2779	
  2780	    private fun drawMockCreateUsername(
  2781	        canvas: Canvas,
  2782	        centerX: Float,
  2783	        baselineY: Float,
  2784	        stageScale: Float,
  2785	        alpha: Float
  2786	    ) {
  2787	        if (usernameText.isEmpty()) return
  2788	
  2789	        val maxTextWidth =
  2790	            (MOCK_FORM_WIDTH - MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_MAX_WIDTH_INSET * 2f) * stageScale
  2791	        val preferredTextSize = MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2792	        createUsernameFillPaint.textSize = preferredTextSize
  2793	        val preferredTextWidth = createUsernameFillPaint.measureText(usernameText)
  2794	        val fittedTextSize = if (preferredTextWidth > maxTextWidth && preferredTextWidth > 0f) {
  2795	            preferredTextSize * maxTextWidth / preferredTextWidth
  2796	        } else {
  2797	            preferredTextSize
  2798	        }
  2799	        val paintAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  2800	
  2801	        createUsernameStrokePaint.textSize = fittedTextSize
  2802	        createUsernameStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2803	        createUsernameStrokePaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  2804	        createUsernameStrokePaint.setShadowLayer(
  2805	            MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_SHADOW_RADIUS * stageScale,
  2806	            0f,
  2807	            MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_SHADOW_OFFSET_Y * stageScale,
  2808	            Color.argb((paintAlpha * 0.25f).roundToInt(), 0, 0, 0)
  2809	        )
  2810	        canvas.drawText(usernameText, centerX, baselineY, createUsernameStrokePaint)
  2811	        createUsernameStrokePaint.clearShadowLayer()
  2812	
  2813	        createUsernameFillPaint.textSize = fittedTextSize
  2814	        createUsernameFillPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  2815	        canvas.drawText(usernameText, centerX, baselineY, createUsernameFillPaint)
  2816	
  2817	        createUsernameStrokePaint.alpha = 255
  2818	        createUsernameFillPaint.alpha = 255
  2819	    }

  2821	    private fun drawMockCreateBirthdayLabel(
  2822	        canvas: Canvas,
  2823	        columnX: Float,
  2824	        labelTopY: Float,
  2825	        stageLeft: Float,
  2826	        stageTop: Float,

  2832	        formLabelPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  2833	        val metrics = formLabelPaint.fontMetrics
  2834	        canvas.drawText(

  2836	            stageLeft + (columnX + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f) * stageScale,
  2837	            stageTop + labelTopY * stageScale - metrics.ascent,
  2838	            formLabelPaint
  2839	        )
  2840	    }
  2841	
  2842	    private fun drawMockCreateAvatarMotionLayer(
  2843	        canvas: Canvas,
  2844	        bitmaps: List<Bitmap>,
  2845	        bounds: Bounds,
  2846	        stageLeft: Float,
  2847	        stageTop: Float,
  2848	        stageScale: Float,
  2849	        alpha: Float,
  2850	        transitionProgressOverride: Float? = null
  2851	    ) {
  2852	        val carouselProgress = transitionProgressOverride ?: mockCreateAvatarCarouselProgress()
  2853	        val slideDirection = mockCreateAvatarCarouselDirection.stageDirection
  2854	        val slideDistance = mockCreateAvatarSlideDistance(bounds.w)
  2855	        val dragDirection = mockCreateAvatarDragDirection()
  2856	        val dragProgress = transitionProgressOverride ?:
  2857	            mockCreateAvatarDragProgress(slideDistance, stageScale)
  2858	        val isDraggingAvatar = activeMockCreateAvatarDrag &&
  2859	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved &&
  2860	            dragDirection != null &&
  2861	            !isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()
  2862	
  2863	        fun draw(index: Int, offsetX: Float) {
  2864	            drawFigmaBitmap(
  2865	                canvas,
  2866	                bitmaps[index.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)],
  2867	                Bounds(bounds.x + offsetX, bounds.y, bounds.w, bounds.h),
  2868	                stageLeft,
  2869	                stageTop,
  2870	                stageScale,
  2871	                alpha
  2872	            )
  2873	        }
  2874	
  2875	        if (isDraggingAvatar && dragDirection != null) {
  2876	            val direction = dragDirection.stageDirection
  2877	            draw(mockCreateAvatarIndex, -direction * dragProgress * slideDistance)
  2878	            draw(mockCreateAvatarIndexForDirection(dragDirection), direction * (1f - dragProgress) * slideDistance)
  2879	        } else if (isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()) {
  2880	            draw(mockCreateAvatarCarouselFromIndex, -slideDirection * carouselProgress * slideDistance)
  2881	            draw(mockCreateAvatarCarouselToIndex, slideDirection * (1f - carouselProgress) * slideDistance)
  2882	        } else {
  2883	            draw(mockCreateAvatarIndex, 0f)
  2884	        }
  2885	    }
  2886	
  2887	    private fun clipMockCreateAvatarCarousel(
  2888	        canvas: Canvas,
  2889	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  2890	        stageLeft: Float,
  2891	        stageTop: Float,
  2892	        stageScale: Float
  2893	    ) {
  2894	        tempPath.reset()
  2895	        tempPath.addCircle(
  2896	            stageLeft + (avatarBounds.x + avatarBounds.w / 2f) * stageScale,
  2897	            stageTop + (avatarBounds.y + avatarBounds.h / 2f) * stageScale,
  2898	            avatarBounds.w * 0.5f * stageScale,
  2899	            Path.Direction.CW
  2900	        )
  2901	        canvas.clipPath(tempPath)
  2902	    }
  2903	
  2904	    private fun drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2905	        canvas: Canvas,
  2906	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  2907	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  2908	        stageLeft: Float,
  2909	        stageTop: Float,
  2910	        stageScale: Float,
  2911	        alpha: Float,
  2912	        centerXOverridePx: Float? = null
  2913	    ) {
  2914	        val centerX = centerXOverridePx ?:
  2915	            stageLeft + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterX(direction, avatarBounds) * stageScale
  2916	        val centerY = stageTop + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterY(avatarBounds) * stageScale
  2917	        val halfWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  2918	        val halfHeight = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HEIGHT * stageScale / 2f
  2919	        val pressedAlpha = if (pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == direction) 0.62f else 0.9f
  2920	        backChevronPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * pressedAlpha * 255f).roundToInt()
  2921	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2922	
  2923	        if (direction == MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous) {
  2924	            canvas.drawLine(centerX + halfWidth, centerY - halfHeight, centerX - halfWidth, centerY, backChevronPaint)
  2925	            canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfWidth, centerY, centerX + halfWidth, centerY + halfHeight, backChevronPaint)
  2926	        } else {
  2927	            canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfWidth, centerY - halfHeight, centerX + halfWidth, centerY, backChevronPaint)
  2928	            canvas.drawLine(centerX + halfWidth, centerY, centerX - halfWidth, centerY + halfHeight, backChevronPaint)
  2929	        }
  2930	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  2931	    }
  2932	
  2933	    private fun drawMockDropdownControl(
  2934	        canvas: Canvas,
  2935	        dropdown: MockDropdown,
  2936	        x: Float,
  2937	        y: Float,
  2938	        stageLeft: Float,
  2939	        stageTop: Float,
  2940	        stageScale: Float,
  2941	        alpha: Float,
  2942	        dropdownWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  2943	    ) {
  2944	        tempRect.set(
  2945	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  2946	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  2947	            stageLeft + (x + dropdownWidth) * stageScale,
  2948	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  2949	        )
  2950	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  2951	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2952	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2953	
  2954	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  2955	        formInputPaint.textSize = mockDropdownTextSize(dropdown) * stageScale
  2956	        formInputPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2957	        formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2958	        val textInset = mockDropdownTextInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  2959	        val chevronInset = mockDropdownChevronInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  2960	        val displayText = mockDropdownDisplayText(dropdown)
  2961	        val membershipIcon = membershipTierArtworkIconForOption(displayText)
  2962	            ?.takeIf { dropdown == MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier }
  2963	        val iconLaneWidth = if (membershipIcon != null) {
  2964	            (MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_CONTROL_ICON_SIZE + MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_ICON_TEXT_GAP) * stageScale
  2965	        } else {
  2966	            0f
  2967	        }
  2968	        val maxTextWidth = (dropdownWidth * stageScale - textInset - iconLaneWidth - chevronInset * 2f)
  2969	            .coerceAtLeast(0f)
  2970	        if (membershipIcon != null) {
  2971	            drawMembershipTierIcon(
  2972	                canvas = canvas,
  2973	                bitmap = membershipIcon,
  2974	                left = tempRect.left + textInset,
  2975	                centerY = tempRect.centerY(),
  2976	                iconSize = MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_CONTROL_ICON_SIZE * stageScale,
  2977	                alpha = textAlpha
  2978	            )
  2979	        }
  2980	        canvas.drawText(
  2981	            trailingFittingText(displayText, maxTextWidth, formInputPaint),
  2982	            tempRect.left + textInset + iconLaneWidth,
  2983	            tempRect.centerY() - (formInputPaint.fontMetrics.ascent + formInputPaint.fontMetrics.descent) / 2f,
  2984	            formInputPaint
  2985	        )
  2986	
  2987	        val centerX = tempRect.right - chevronInset
  2988	        val centerY = tempRect.centerY() + 2f * stageScale
  2989	        val halfSize = mockDropdownChevronSize(dropdown) * stageScale / 2f
  2990	        backChevronPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2991	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = 1.65f * stageScale
  2992	        canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfSize, centerY - halfSize / 2f, centerX, centerY + halfSize / 2f, backChevronPaint)
  2993	        canvas.drawLine(centerX, centerY + halfSize / 2f, centerX + halfSize, centerY - halfSize / 2f, backChevronPaint)
  2994	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  2995	    }

  2997	    private fun drawMockDropdownOptions(
  2998	        canvas: Canvas,
  2999	        stageLeft: Float,
  3000	        stageTop: Float,
  3001	        stageScale: Float,
  3002	        alpha: Float
  3003	    ) {
  3004	        val dropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: return

  3014	        val top = stageTop + y * stageScale

  3020	            (MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * 2f) * stageScale

  3027	            val optionTop = top + index * optionHeight

  3030	                Color.argb(optionAlpha, 76, 12, 94)
  3031	            } else {

  3033	            }

  3085	    private fun drawMembershipTierIcon(
  3086	        canvas: Canvas,
  3087	        bitmap: Bitmap,
  3088	        left: Float,
  3089	        centerY: Float,
  3090	        iconSize: Float,
  3091	        alpha: Int
  3092	    ) {
  3093	        val bitmapAspect = bitmap.width.toFloat() / bitmap.height.toFloat()
  3094	        val width: Float
  3095	        val height: Float
  3096	        if (bitmapAspect >= 1f) {
  3097	            width = iconSize
  3098	            height = iconSize / bitmapAspect
  3099	        } else {
  3100	            width = iconSize * bitmapAspect
  3101	            height = iconSize
  3102	        }
  3103	        val top = centerY - height / 2f
  3104	        tempIconRect.set(left, top, left + width, top + height)
  3105	        imagePaint.alpha = alpha.coerceIn(0, 255)
  3106	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempIconRect, imagePaint)
  3107	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  3108	    }

  3110	    private fun drawMockDropdownScrollbar(
  3111	        canvas: Canvas,
  3112	        dropdown: MockDropdown,
  3113	        top: Float,
  3114	        right: Float,
  3115	        dropdownHeight: Float,
  3116	        stageScale: Float,
  3117	        alpha: Int
  3118	    ) {
  3119	        val options = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown)
  3120	        val visibleCount = mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)
  3121	        val maxOffset = mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown)
  3122	        if (options.isEmpty() || maxOffset <= 0) return
  3123	
  3124	        val trackRight = right - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * stageScale
  3125	        val trackLeft = trackRight - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH * stageScale
  3126	        val trackTop = top + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_Y * stageScale
  3127	        val trackBottom = top + dropdownHeight - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_Y * stageScale
  3128	        val trackHeight = (trackBottom - trackTop).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  3129	        val thumbHeight = maxOf(14f * stageScale, trackHeight * visibleCount / options.size)
  3130	            .coerceAtMost(trackHeight)
  3131	        val thumbProgress = mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown) / maxOffset.toFloat()
  3132	        val thumbTop = trackTop + (trackHeight - thumbHeight) * thumbProgress
  3133	        val radius = MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  3134

  3136	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 0.28f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  3137	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  3138	
  3139	        tempRect.set(trackLeft, thumbTop, trackRight, thumbTop + thumbHeight)
  3140	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 0.78f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  3141	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)

  3199

  3201	            val inputBaselineY = tempRect.top + MOCK_FORM_INPUT_BASELINE_FROM_TOP * stageScale

  3205

  3331	    private fun decode(resId: Int): Bitmap {

  3334	
  3335	    private data class CreateAvatarLayers(
  3336	        val bubble: Bitmap,
  3337	        val characters: List<Bitmap>,
  3338	        val bottomStrokes: List<Bitmap>
  3339	    )
  3340	
  3341	    private fun createAvatarCarouselLayers(vararg avatars: Bitmap): CreateAvatarLayers {
  3342	        require(avatars.size == MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT)
  3343	        val bitmapWidth = avatars.first().width
  3344	        val bitmapHeight = avatars.first().height
  3345	        require(avatars.all { it.width == bitmapWidth && it.height == bitmapHeight })
  3346	        return CreateAvatarLayers(
  3347	            bubble = createAvatarBubbleShellBitmap(avatars.first()),
  3348	            characters = avatars.map(::createAvatarCharacterBitmap),
  3349	            bottomStrokes = avatars.map(::createAvatarBottomStrokeBitmap)
  3350	        )
  3351	    }
  3352	
  3353	    private fun createAvatarBottomStrokeBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  3354	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  3355	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  3356	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  3357	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  3358	        val strokePixels = IntArray(pixels.size)
  3359	        val minimumBodyY = (bitmapHeight * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BOTTOM_STROKE_START_RATIO).roundToInt()
  3360	
  3361	        for (x in 0 until bitmapWidth) {
  3362	            var bodyBottomY = -1
  3363	            for (y in minimumBodyY until bitmapHeight) {
  3364	                if (isAvatarCharacterFillPixel(pixels[y * bitmapWidth + x])) bodyBottomY = y
  3365	            }
  3366	            if (bodyBottomY < 0) continue
  3367	            val strokeBottomY = minOf(bitmapHeight - 1, bodyBottomY + MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BOTTOM_STROKE_DEPTH_PX)
  3368	            for (y in bodyBottomY + 1..strokeBottomY) {
  3369	                val index = y * bitmapWidth + x
  3370	                val sourceAlpha = Color.alpha(pixels[index])
  3371	                if (sourceAlpha <= 16) continue
  3372	                strokePixels[index] = Color.argb(
  3373	                    sourceAlpha,
  3374	                    MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STROKE_CHANNEL,
  3375	                    MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STROKE_CHANNEL,
  3376	                    MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STROKE_CHANNEL
  3377	                )
  3378	            }
  3379	        }
  3380	        val cornerFilledStrokePixels = strokePixels.copyOf()
  3381	        for (y in minimumBodyY until bitmapHeight) {
  3382	            val rowStart = y * bitmapWidth
  3383	            for (x in 0 until bitmapWidth) {
  3384	                if (strokePixels[rowStart + x] == Color.TRANSPARENT) continue
  3385	                val sideStart = maxOf(0, x - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BOTTOM_STROKE_SIDE_EXTEND_PX)
  3386	                val sideEnd = minOf(bitmapWidth - 1, x + MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BOTTOM_STROKE_SIDE_EXTEND_PX)
  3387	                for (sideX in sideStart..sideEnd) {
  3388	                    val index = rowStart + sideX
  3389	                    val sourceAlpha = Color.alpha(pixels[index])
  3390	                    if (sourceAlpha <= 16 || isAvatarCharacterFillPixel(pixels[index])) continue
  3391	                    cornerFilledStrokePixels[index] = Color.argb(
  3392	                        sourceAlpha,
  3393	                        MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STROKE_CHANNEL,
  3394	                        MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STROKE_CHANNEL,
  3395	                        MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STROKE_CHANNEL
  3396	                    )
  3397	                }
  3398	            }
  3399	        }
  3400	        return bitmapFromPixels(cornerFilledStrokePixels, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  3401	    }
  3402	
  3403	    private fun isAvatarCharacterFillPixel(pixel: Int): Boolean {
  3404	        if (Color.alpha(pixel) <= 16) return false
  3405	        val channelMax = maxOf(Color.red(pixel), Color.green(pixel), Color.blue(pixel))
  3406	        val channelMin = minOf(Color.red(pixel), Color.green(pixel), Color.blue(pixel))
  3407	        return channelMax > 40 && channelMax - channelMin > 8
  3408	    }
  3409	
  3410	    private fun createAvatarBubbleShellBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  3411	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  3412	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  3413	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  3414	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  3415	
  3416	        val centerX = (bitmapWidth - 1f) / 2f
  3417	        val centerY = (bitmapHeight - 1f) / 2f
  3418	        val innerRadius = min(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight) *
  3419	            (0.5f - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BUBBLE_INNER_INSET_RATIO)
  3420	        val innerRadiusSquared = innerRadius * innerRadius
  3421	        val shellPixels = IntArray(pixels.size) { index ->
  3422	            val x = index % bitmapWidth
  3423	            val y = index / bitmapWidth
  3424	            val dx = x - centerX
  3425	            val dy = y - centerY
  3426	            if (dx * dx + dy * dy >= innerRadiusSquared) pixels[index] else Color.TRANSPARENT
  3427	        }
  3428	        return bitmapFromPixels(shellPixels, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  3429	    }
  3430	
  3431	    private fun createAvatarCharacterBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  3432	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  3433	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  3434	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  3435	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  3436	
  3437	        val centerX = (bitmapWidth - 1f) / 2f
  3438	        val centerY = (bitmapHeight - 1f) / 2f
  3439	        val innerRadius = min(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight) * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CLIP_RADIUS_RATIO
  3440	        val innerRadiusSquared = innerRadius * innerRadius
  3441	        val distance = IntArray(pixels.size) { -1 }
  3442	        val queue = IntArray(pixels.size)
  3443	        var queueStart = 0
  3444	        var queueEnd = 0
  3445	
  3446	        pixels.indices.forEach { index ->
  3447	            val x = index % bitmapWidth
  3448	            val y = index / bitmapWidth
  3449	            val dx = x - centerX
  3450	            val dy = y - centerY
  3451	            if (dx * dx + dy * dy < innerRadiusSquared && isAvatarCharacterColor(pixels[index])) {
  3452	                distance[index] = 0
  3453	                queue[queueEnd++] = index
  3454	            }
  3455	        }
  3456	
  3457	        while (queueStart < queueEnd) {
  3458	            val index = queue[queueStart++]
  3459	            val currentDistance = distance[index]
  3460	            if (currentDistance >= MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_OUTLINE_RADIUS_PX) continue
  3461	            val x = index % bitmapWidth
  3462	            val y = index / bitmapWidth
  3463	            for (dy in -1..1) {
  3464	                val nextY = y + dy
  3465	                if (nextY !in 0 until bitmapHeight) continue
  3466	                for (dx in -1..1) {
  3467	                    if (dx == 0 && dy == 0) continue
  3468	                    val nextX = x + dx
  3469	                    if (nextX !in 0 until bitmapWidth) continue
  3470	                    val nextIndex = nextY * bitmapWidth + nextX
  3471	                    if (distance[nextIndex] >= 0 || !isAvatarOutlinePixel(pixels[nextIndex])) continue
  3472	                    val centerDx = nextX - centerX
  3473	                    val centerDy = nextY - centerY
  3474	                    if (centerDx * centerDx + centerDy * centerDy >= innerRadiusSquared) continue
  3475	                    distance[nextIndex] = currentDistance + 1
  3476	                    queue[queueEnd++] = nextIndex
  3477	                }
  3478	            }
  3479	        }
  3480	
  3481	        val characterPixels = IntArray(pixels.size) { index ->
  3482	            if (distance[index] >= 0) pixels[index] else Color.TRANSPARENT
  3483	        }
  3484	        return bitmapFromPixels(characterPixels, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  3485	    }
  3486	
  3487	    private fun isAvatarCharacterColor(pixel: Int): Boolean {
  3488	        if (Color.alpha(pixel) <= 16) return false
  3489	        val red = Color.red(pixel)
  3490	        val green = Color.green(pixel)
  3491	        val blue = Color.blue(pixel)
  3492	        val channelMax = maxOf(red, green, blue)
  3493	        val channelMin = minOf(red, green, blue)
  3494	        val isNeutralBubbleOrRing = channelMax - channelMin <= 4
  3495	        return (Color.alpha(pixel) >= 250 && !isNeutralBubbleOrRing) ||
  3496	            (channelMax > 40 && channelMax - channelMin > 8)
  3497	    }
  3498	
  3499	    private fun isAvatarOutlinePixel(pixel: Int): Boolean {
  3500	        return Color.alpha(pixel) > 24 && maxOf(Color.red(pixel), Color.green(pixel), Color.blue(pixel)) <= 80
  3501	    }
  3502	
  3503	    private fun bitmapFromPixels(pixels: IntArray, width: Int, height: Int): Bitmap {
  3504	        return Bitmap.createBitmap(width, height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888).apply {
  3505	            setPixels(pixels, 0, width, 0, 0, width, height)
  3506	        }
  3507	    }
  3508	
  3509	    private fun standardSignInBackgroundForSize(): Bitmap? {
  3510	        return when {
  3511	            isSizeCloseTo(width, STANDARD_TABLET_LONG_EDGE) &&
  3512	                isSizeCloseTo(height, STANDARD_TABLET_SHORT_EDGE) -> standardLandscapeBackground
  3513	            isSizeCloseTo(width, STANDARD_TABLET_SHORT_EDGE) &&
  3514	                isSizeCloseTo(height, STANDARD_TABLET_LONG_EDGE) -> standardPortraitBackground
  3515	            else -> null
  3516	        }
  3517	    }
  3518	
  3519	    private fun isSizeCloseTo(actual: Int, expected: Int): Boolean {
  3520	        return abs(actual - expected) <= STANDARD_TABLET_SIZE_TOLERANCE
  3521	    }
  3522	
  3523	    private fun isFinalFrameSettled(): Boolean {
  3524	        return IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis > 0L &&
  3525	            bootProgressAt(IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis) >= 1f
  3526	    }

  3528	    private fun settledAvatarHitProfile(x: Float, y: Float): CinerificProfile? {
  3529	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) return null
  3530	        if (!isFinalFrameSettled()) return null
  3531	
  3532	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  3533	
  3534	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  3535	            val avatarSize = portraitAvatarSize()
  3536	            val centerX = stage.left + FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
  3537	            val halfWidth = (avatarSize / 2f + PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SWIPE_HIT_HORIZONTAL_PADDING) * stage.scale
  3538	            val top = stage.top + portraitAvatarTop() * stage.scale
  3539	            val bottom = stage.top + (
  3540	                portraitProfileNameTop() + portraitProfileNameHeight() +
  3541	                    PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SWIPE_HIT_BOTTOM_PADDING
  3542	                ) * stage.scale
  3543	            return if (x in (centerX - halfWidth)..(centerX + halfWidth) && y in top..bottom) {
  3544	                FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS[portraitProfileIndex].profile
  3545	            } else {
  3546	                null
  3547	            }
  3548	        }
  3549	
  3550	        val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY()
  3551	        return FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.firstOrNull { target ->
  3552	            val bounds = target.bounds
  3553	            val centerX = stage.left + (bounds.x + bounds.w / 2f) * stage.scale
  3554	            val centerY = stage.top + (bounds.y + stackShiftY + bounds.h / 2f) * stage.scale
  3555	            val radius = min(bounds.w, bounds.h) * stage.scale / 2f
  3556	            val dx = x - centerX
  3557	            val dy = y - centerY
  3558	            dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius
  3559	        }?.profile
  3560	    }

  3785	    private fun updateMockCreateAvatarDrag(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  3786	        if (!activeMockCreateAvatarDrag) return false
  3787	
  3788	        mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
  3789	        val dragDeltaY = event.y - mockTouchDownY
  3790	        if (
  3791	            !mockCreateAvatarDragMoved &&
  3792	            abs(mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX) > touchSlop &&
  3793	            abs(mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX) > abs(dragDeltaY)
  3794	        ) {
  3795	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = true
  3796	            lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = if (mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX < 0f) {
  3797	                MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next
  3798	            } else {
  3799	                MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous
  3800	            }
  3801	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  3802	        }
  3803	
  3804	        if (mockCreateAvatarDragMoved) {
  3805	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3806	        }
  3807	        return true
  3808	    }

  3810	    private fun mockDropdownOptionHit(pointerX: Float, pointerY: Float): MockDropdownOption? {
  3811	        if (
  3812	            mockSignInStartMillis == null ||
  3813	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount ||
  3814	            expandedMockDropdown == null
  3815	        ) {
  3816	            return null
  3817	        }
  3818	
  3819	        val dropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: return null
  3820	        val baseStage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  3821	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  3822	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  3823	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  3824	        if (!portraitAuthPointerInViewport(pointerY, baseStage)) return null
  3825	        val stage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(baseStage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  3826	        val dropdownX = mockDropdownX(dropdown)
  3827	        val yStart = mockDropdownOptionsY(dropdown, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  3828	        val left = stage.left + dropdownX * stage.scale
  3829	        val right = stage.left + (dropdownX + mockDropdownWidth(dropdown)) * stage.scale
  3830	        if (pointerX !in left..right) return null
  3831	        if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  3832	            val scrollbarLeft = right -
  3833	                (MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * 2f) * stage.scale
  3834	            if (pointerX >= scrollbarLeft) return null
  3835	        }
  3836	
  3837	        mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown).forEachIndexed { optionIndex, option ->
  3838	            val optionHeight = mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown)
  3839	            val top = stage.top + (yStart + optionIndex * optionHeight) * stage.scale
  3840	            val bottom = top + optionHeight * stage.scale
  3841	            if (pointerY in top..bottom) return MockDropdownOption(dropdown, option)
  3842	        }
  3843	        return null
  3844	    }

  3871	    private fun updateMockDropdownScroll(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  3872	        val dropdown = activeMockDropdownScroll ?: return false
  3873	        if (expandedMockDropdown != dropdown || !mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) return false
  3874	
  3875	        val baseStage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return true
  3876	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  3877	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  3878	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  3879	        val stage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(baseStage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  3880	        val optionHeightPx = (mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stage.scale).coerceAtLeast(1f)
  3881	        val dragDeltaY = event.y - mockDropdownScrollStartY
  3882	        if (abs(dragDeltaY) > touchSlop) {
  3883	            mockDropdownScrollMoved = true
  3884	            pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  3885	        }
  3886	
  3887	        if (mockDropdownScrollMoved) {
  3888	            val rowDelta = ((mockDropdownScrollStartY - event.y) / optionHeightPx).roundToInt()
  3889	            setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, mockDropdownScrollStartOffset + rowDelta)
  3890	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3891	        }
  3892	        return true
  3893	    }

  3895	    private fun updatePortraitAuthScroll(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  3896	        if (
  3897	            !isPortraitIntroLayout() ||
  3898	            activeMockFlow !in listOf(
  3899	                MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount,
  3900	                MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword
  3901	            ) ||
  3902	            activeMockDropdownScroll != null
  3903	        ) {
  3904	            return false
  3905	        }
  3906	
  3907	        val deltaX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
  3908	        val deltaY = event.y - portraitAuthScrollStartY
  3909	        if (!portraitAuthScrollMoved) {
  3910	            if (abs(deltaY) <= touchSlop || abs(deltaY) <= abs(deltaX)) return false
  3911	            if (mockDragReturnEligibleAtTouchDown && deltaY < 0f) return false
  3912	            mockDragReturnEligibleAtTouchDown = false
  3913	            portraitAuthScrollMoved = true
  3914	            pressedMockField = null
  3915	            pressedMockDropdown = null
  3916	            pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  3917	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  3918	            activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
  3919	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
  3920	            expandedMockDropdown = null
  3921	            clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  3922	        }
  3923	
  3924	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return true
  3925	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  3926	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  3927	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  3928	        val maxScrollY = when (activeMockFlow) {
  3929	            MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword -> portraitForgotPasswordMaxScrollY(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  3930	            else -> portraitCreateMaxScrollY(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  3931	        }
  3932	        portraitAuthScrollY = (portraitAuthScrollStartOffset - deltaY).coerceIn(0f, maxScrollY)
  3933	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3934	        return true
  3935	    }
  3936	
  3937	    private fun mockBackChevronHit(x: Float, y: Float): Boolean {
  3938	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null || isMockSignInClosing()) return false
  3939	
  3940	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return false
  3941	        if (activeMockFlow == null) return false
  3942	        return mockBackChevronContains(
  3943	            x,
  3944	            y,
  3945	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X,
  3946	            mockBackChevronRowTopY(),
  3947	            stage
  3948	        )
  3949	    }
  3950	
  3951	    private fun shouldStartMockDragReturn(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  3952	        if (
  3953	            mockDragReturnInProgress ||
  3954	            isMockSignInClosing() ||
  3955	            !mockDragReturnEligibleAtTouchDown
  3956	        ) {
  3957	            return false
  3958	        }
  3959	
  3960	        val downwardScrollY = mockTouchDownY - event.y
  3961	        if (downwardScrollY <= mockBackDragThresholdPx()) return false
  3962	
  3963	        val dragX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
  3964	        return downwardScrollY > abs(dragX)
  3965	    }
  3966	
  3967	    private fun isMockAuthScreenAtBottom(): Boolean {
  3968	        if (activeMockFlow == null) return false
  3969	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) return true
  3970	
  3971	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return false
  3972	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  3973	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  3974	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(
  3975	            FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress)
  3976	        )
  3977	        val maxScrollY = when (activeMockFlow) {
  3978	            MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount -> portraitCreateMaxScrollY(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  3979	            MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword -> portraitForgotPasswordMaxScrollY(
  3980	                stage,
  3981	                yOffset,
  3982	                focusShiftY
  3983	            )
  3984	            MockAccountFlow.SignIn -> 0f
  3985	            null -> return false
  3986	        }
  3987	        return portraitAuthScrollY >= maxScrollY - maxOf(1f, stage.scale)
  3988	    }

  3990	    private fun mockBackDragThresholdPx(): Float {
  3991	        val stageThreshold = currentStageMetrics()?.let { stage ->
  3992	            MOCK_BACK_DRAG_THRESHOLD * stage.scale
  3993	        } ?: MOCK_BACK_DRAG_THRESHOLD
  3994	        return maxOf(stageThreshold, touchSlop * 3f)
  3995	    }

  4008	        val bottom = stage.top + (fieldY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) * stage.scale

  4010	    }

  4072	    private fun mockBackChevronContains(
  4073	        pointerX: Float,
  4074	        pointerY: Float,
  4075	        centerX: Float,
  4076	        topY: Float,
  4077	        stage: StageMetrics
  4078	    ): Boolean {
  4079	        val chevronScale = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  4080	            MOCK_PORTRAIT_STICKY_BOTTOM_CHEVRON_SCALE
  4081	        } else {
  4082	            1f
  4083	        }
  4084	        val center = stage.left + centerX * stage.scale
  4085	        val hitHalfWidth = MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_HIT_WIDTH * chevronScale * stage.scale / 2f
  4086	        val left = center - hitHalfWidth
  4087	        val right = center + hitHalfWidth
  4088	        val backStageTop = if (usesPortraitStackedAuthLayout()) {
  4089	            stage.top
  4090	        } else {
  4091	            accountPromptStageTop(stage.scale)
  4092	        }

  4094	            (topY - (MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_HIT_HEIGHT - MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_HEIGHT) *
  4095	                chevronScale / 2f) * stage.scale
  4096	        val bottom = top + MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_HIT_HEIGHT * chevronScale * stage.scale
  4097	        return pointerX in left..right && pointerY in top..bottom
  4098	    }

  4129	        val contentCenterY = (contentTop + contentBottom) / 2f

  4131	            (MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_SIDE_GAP + MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HIT_WIDTH / 2f) * 2f
  4132	        val side = maxOf(chevronSpan, contentBottom - contentTop)
  4133	        return Bounds(
  4134	            centerX - side / 2f,
  4135	            contentCenterY - side / 2f,
  4136	            side,
  4137	            side
  4138	        )

  4160	    private fun mockCreateAvatarChevronContains(
  4161	        pointerX: Float,

  4164	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  4165	        stage: StageMetrics

  4171	        return pointerX in (centerX - halfWidth)..(centerX + halfWidth) &&
  4172	            pointerY in (centerY - halfHeight)..(centerY + halfHeight)
  4173	    }

  4175	    private fun focusMockSignInField(field: MockSignInField) {
  4176	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = field
  4177	        mockInputDimActiveField = field
  4178	        mockInputDimActiveDropdown = null
  4179	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  4180	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  4181	        if (focusedMockField != field) {
  4182	            activeComposingText = ""
  4183	            focusedMockField = field
  4184	            restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  4185	            restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  4186	        }
  4187	        requestFocus()
  4188	        post {
  4189	            val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  4190	            inputMethodManager?.restartInput(this)
  4191	            inputMethodManager?.showSoftInput(this, InputMethodManager.SHOW_IMPLICIT)
  4192	        }
  4193	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  4194	    }

  4200	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = null

  4207	        clearFocus()
  4208	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()

  4332	    private fun portraitAuthViewportTopPx(stage: StageMetrics): Float {
  4333	        val title = when (activeMockFlow) {
  4334	            MockAccountFlow.SignIn -> ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT
  4335	            MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount -> ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_TEXT
  4336	            MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword -> ACCOUNT_PROMPT_FORGOT_TEXT
  4337	            null -> null
  4338	        }
  4339	        if (title != null) {
  4340	            val titleTextSize = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE * PORTRAIT_ACTION_SCALE * stage.scale
  4341	            val titleBaseline = mockFormTitleTargetBaseline(
  4342	                title,
  4343	                mockPrimaryFormY(),
  4344	                stage.top,
  4345	                stage.scale,
  4346	                titleTextSize
  4347	            )
  4348	            formTitlePaint.textSize = titleTextSize
  4349	            return titleBaseline + formTitlePaint.fontMetrics.descent +
  4350	                mockFormFieldGap() * stage.scale * portraitCreateContentScale()
  4351	        }
  4352	        return stage.top +
  4353	            (portraitLogoVisibleBottom() + MOCK_CREATE_PORTRAIT_VIEWPORT_GAP) * stage.scale
  4354	    }

  4361	            stickyBottomHeight * portraitStickyBottomMotion()
  4362	    }

  4386	
  4387	    private fun portraitCreateMaxScrollY(
  4388	        stage: StageMetrics,
  4389	        yOffset: Float,
  4390	        focusShiftY: Float
  4391	    ): Float {
  4392	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) return 0f
  4393	        val contentStage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4394	        val avatarSectionY = mockCreateAvatarSectionY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4395	        val avatarBottomY = mockCreateAvatarBounds(
  4396	            mockCreateAvatarColumnX(),
  4397	            avatarSectionY
  4398	        ).let { it.y + it.h }
  4399	        val finalControlBottomY = mockDropdownY(
  4400	            MockDropdown.Year,
  4401	            yOffset,
  4402	            focusShiftY
  4403	        ) + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT
  4404	        val contentBottomY = maxOf(avatarBottomY, finalControlBottomY)
  4405	        val contentBottom = contentStage.top + contentBottomY * contentStage.scale +
  4406	            MOCK_CREATE_PORTRAIT_CONTENT_BOTTOM_GAP * stage.scale + portraitAuthScrollY
  4407	        return (contentBottom - portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(stage)).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  4408	    }
  4409	
  4410	    private fun portraitForgotPasswordMaxScrollY(
  4411	        stage: StageMetrics,
  4412	        yOffset: Float,
  4413	        focusShiftY: Float
  4414	    ): Float {
  4415	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) return 0f
  4416	        val contentStage = mockForgotPasswordContentStageMetrics(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4417	        val helpBottomY = mockForgotPasswordHelpBottomY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4418	        val contentBottom = contentStage.top + helpBottomY * contentStage.scale +
  4419	            MOCK_CREATE_PORTRAIT_CONTENT_BOTTOM_GAP * stage.scale + portraitAuthScrollY
  4420	        return (contentBottom - portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(stage)).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  4421	    }

  4497	    private fun mockForgotPasswordBodyBottomY(formTopY: Float): Float {
  4498	        val paragraphs = mockForgotPasswordBodyText().split(Regex("\\n\\s*\\n"))
  4499	        val copyWidth = mockForgotPasswordCopyWidth()
  4500	        var bottomY = mockParagraphBottomY(
  4501	            paragraphs.firstOrNull().orEmpty(),
  4502	            formTopY + mockForgotPasswordBodyTopGap(),
  4503	            copyWidth,
  4504	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_TEXT_SIZE,
  4505	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_LINE_HEIGHT,
  4506	            forceLineBreaks = true
  4507	        )
  4508	        paragraphs.getOrNull(1)?.let { middleText ->
  4509	            bottomY = mockParagraphBottomY(
  4510	                middleText,
  4511	                bottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  4512	                copyWidth,
  4513	                MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  4514	                MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT
  4515	            )
  4516	        }
  4517	        val bottomText = paragraphs.drop(2).joinToString("\n\n")
  4518	        if (bottomText.isNotEmpty()) {
  4519	            bottomY = mockParagraphBottomY(
  4520	                bottomText,
  4521	                bottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  4522	                copyWidth,
  4523	                MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  4524	                MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT
  4525	            )
  4526	        }
  4527	        return bottomY
  4528	    }
  4529

  4533	        } else {
  4534	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_LANDSCAPE_BODY_TOP_GAP
  4535	        }

  4538	    private fun mockForgotPasswordHelpBottomY(yOffset: Float, focusShiftY: Float): Float {

  4540	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_HEIGHT + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_HELP_TOP_GAP
  4541	        return mockParagraphBottomY(
  4542	            mockForgotPasswordHelpText(),
  4543	            helpTopY,
  4544	            mockForgotPasswordFormWidth(),
  4545	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_HELP_TEXT_SIZE,
  4546	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_HELP_LINE_HEIGHT
  4547	        )
  4548	    }
  4549	
  4550	    private fun mockParagraphBottomY(
  4551	        text: String,
  4552	        topY: Float,
  4553	        maxWidth: Float,
  4554	        textSize: Float,
  4555	        lineHeight: Float,
  4556	        forceLineBreaks: Boolean = false
  4557	    ): Float {
  4558	        formInputPaint.textSize = textSize
  4559	        val lineCount = if (forceLineBreaks) {
  4560	            text.split('\n').sumOf { line ->
  4561	                wrappedTextLines(line, maxWidth, formInputPaint).size
  4562	            }
  4563	        } else {
  4564	            wrappedTextLines(text, maxWidth, formInputPaint).size
  4565	        }
  4566	        return topY + lineCount * lineHeight
  4567	    }

  4569	    private fun mockForgotRecoverySelectorLayout(
  4570	        left: Float,
  4571	        width: Float,
  4572	        labelWidth: Float,
  4573	        boxWidth: Float
  4574	    ): ForgotRecoverySelectorLayout {
  4575	        val gap = ((width - labelWidth - boxWidth * 2f) / 4f).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  4576	        val labelX = left + gap
  4577	        val usernameBoxX = labelX + labelWidth + gap
  4578	        val passwordBoxX = usernameBoxX + boxWidth + gap
  4579	        return ForgotRecoverySelectorLayout(
  4580	            labelX = labelX,
  4581	            usernameBoxX = usernameBoxX,
  4582	            passwordBoxX = passwordBoxX,
  4583	            boxWidth = boxWidth
  4584	        )
  4585	    }
  4586	
  4587	    private fun mockForgotRecoverySelectorBoxWidth(
  4588	        usernameTextWidth: Float,
  4589	        passwordTextWidth: Float,
  4590	        baseBoxWidth: Float = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_BOX_WIDTH
  4591	    ): Float {
  4592	        val widestTextWidth = maxOf(usernameTextWidth, passwordTextWidth)
  4593	        val currentHorizontalPadding = ((baseBoxWidth - widestTextWidth) / 2f).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  4594	        return widestTextWidth + currentHorizontalPadding * 4f
  4595	    }

  4605	    private fun mockActiveFieldX(field: MockSignInField): Float {
  4606	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) return mockForgotPasswordFormX()
  4607	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) return mockFormX()
  4608	
  4609	        val firstColumnX = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) mockCreatePortraitColumnX() else mockCreateFormX()
  4610	        val middleColumnX = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  4611	            mockCreatePortraitColumnX()
  4612	        } else {
  4613	            mockCreateFormX() + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH + MOCK_CREATE_FORM_COLUMN_GAP
  4614	        }
  4615	        return when (field) {
  4616	            MockSignInField.Username,
  4617	            MockSignInField.Password,
  4618	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> firstColumnX
  4619	            MockSignInField.Email -> middleColumnX
  4620	            MockSignInField.Month -> middleColumnX
  4621	            MockSignInField.Day -> middleColumnX + MOCK_DATE_MONTH_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_FIELD_GAP
  4622	            MockSignInField.Year -> middleColumnX + MOCK_DATE_MONTH_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_DAY_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_FIELD_GAP * 2f
  4623	        }
  4624	    }
  4625	
  4626	    private fun mockActiveFieldY(field: MockSignInField, yOffset: Float, focusShiftY: Float): Float {
  4627	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) {
  4628	            return mockForgotPasswordFieldY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4629	        }
  4630	
  4631	        val usesSecondSection = activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount &&
  4632	            isPortraitIntroLayout() &&
  4633	            field in listOf(
  4634	                MockSignInField.Email,
  4635	                MockSignInField.Month,
  4636	                MockSignInField.Day,
  4637	                MockSignInField.Year
  4638	            )
  4639	        val sectionTop = if (usesSecondSection) {
  4640	            mockCreateSecondSectionY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4641	        } else {
  4642	            mockPrimaryFormY() + yOffset + focusShiftY
  4643	        }
  4644	        val fieldOffsetY = if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  4645	            when (field) {
  4646	                MockSignInField.Username -> if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  4647	                    mockCreatePortraitUsernameOffsetY()
  4648	                } else {
  4649	                    0f
  4650	                }
  4651	                MockSignInField.Email -> 0f
  4652	                MockSignInField.Password -> if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  4653	                    mockCreatePortraitPasswordOffsetY()
  4654	                } else {
  4655	                    MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + mockFormFieldGap() * 2f
  4656	                }
  4657	                MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  4658	                    mockCreatePortraitPasswordOffsetY() +
  4659	                        MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + mockFormFieldGap()
  4660	                } else {
  4661	                    MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT * 2f + mockFormFieldGap() * 3f
  4662	                }
  4663	                MockSignInField.Month,
  4664	                MockSignInField.Day,
  4665	                MockSignInField.Year -> mockCreateDateRowOffsetY()
  4666	            }
  4667	        } else {
  4668	            activeMockFields().indexOf(field).coerceAtLeast(0) *
  4669	                (MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + mockFormFieldGap())
  4670	        }
  4671	        return sectionTop + fieldOffsetY
  4672	    }
  4673	
  4674	    private fun mockActiveFieldWidth(field: MockSignInField): Float {

  4693	    private fun mockBackChevronRowTopY(): Float {
  4694	        if (usesPortraitStackedAuthLayout()) {
  4695	            val stickyBottomHeight =
  4696	                MOCK_CREATE_PORTRAIT_VIEWPORT_BOTTOM_RESERVE * MOCK_PORTRAIT_STICKY_BOTTOM_SCALE
  4697	            val chevronHeight =
  4698	                MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_HEIGHT * MOCK_PORTRAIT_STICKY_BOTTOM_CHEVRON_SCALE
  4699	            val settledTop = portraitSafeBottom() - stickyBottomHeight +
  4700	                (stickyBottomHeight - chevronHeight) / 2f
  4701	            return settledTop + stickyBottomHeight * (1f - portraitStickyBottomMotion())
  4702	        }
  4703	        return ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY() +
  4704	            MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_ROW_TOP_GAP
  4705	    }

  4708	        if (!usesPortraitStackedAuthLayout()) return 0f
  4709	        return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4710	    }

  4828	    private fun mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  4829	        val optionCount = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size
  4830	        val viewportCount = if (mockDropdownUsesDateViewport(dropdown)) {
  4831	            MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_VISIBLE_OPTION_COUNT
  4832	        } else {
  4833	            optionCount
  4834	        }
  4835	        return min(optionCount, viewportCount)
  4836	    }
  4837	
  4838	    private fun mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown: MockDropdown): Boolean {
  4839	        return mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size > mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)
  4840	    }
  4841	
  4842	    private fun mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  4843	        return (mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] ?: 0).coerceIn(0, mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown))
  4844	    }
  4845	
  4846	    private fun mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  4847	        return (mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size - mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)).coerceAtLeast(0)
  4848	    }
  4849	
  4850	    private fun setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown, offset: Int) {
  4851	        mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] = offset.coerceIn(0, mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown))
  4852	    }
  4853	
  4854	    private fun mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown: MockDropdown): List<String> {
  4855	        val options = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown)
  4856	        val offset = mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown)
  4857	        return options.drop(offset).take(mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown))
  4858	    }
  4859	
  4860	    private fun prepareMockDropdownScroll(dropdown: MockDropdown) {
  4861	        if (!mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  4862	            setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, 0)
  4863	            return
  4864	        }
  4865	
  4866	        val selectedIndex = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).indexOf(mockDropdownSelectedValue(dropdown))
  4867	        setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, if (selectedIndex >= 0) selectedIndex else 0)
  4868	    }

  4880	            MockDropdown.Month -> monthText

  4895	    private fun setMockDropdownValue(selection: MockDropdownOption) {

  5139	    private fun mockCreateAvatarDragProgress(): Float {
  5140	        val baseStage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return 0f
  5141	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())

  5143	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  5144	        val stage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(baseStage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  5145	        val scaledAvatarSize = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_SIZE * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STACK_SCALE
  5146	        return mockCreateAvatarDragProgress(mockCreateAvatarSlideDistance(scaledAvatarSize), stage.scale)
  5147	    }

  5152	    }

  5196	                mockFieldLabelFloatProgress[field] ?: 0f,

  5295	    private fun mockLandscapeInputLiftTarget(): Float {
  5296	        if (width <= height) return 0f
  5297	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null || isMockSignInClosing()) return 0f
  5298	        return if (
  5299	            focusedMockField != null ||
  5300	            expandedMockDropdown != null ||
  5301	            isSoftKeyboardVisible()
  5302	        ) {
  5303	            1f
  5304	        } else {
  5305	            0f
  5306	        }
  5307	    }
  5308	
  5309	    private fun mockLandscapeInputStageLiftY(stageScale: Float): Float {
  5310	        if (width <= height || mockSignInStartMillis == null) return 0f
  5311	        val liftMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockLandscapeInputLiftProgress)
  5312	        return MOCK_FORM_LANDSCAPE_FOCUS_SHIFT_Y * stageScale * liftMotion
  5313	    }
  5314	
  5315	    private fun isSoftKeyboardVisible(): Boolean {
  5316	        val rootInsets = ViewCompat.getRootWindowInsets(this)
  5317	        if (rootInsets != null) {
  5318	            val imeInsets = rootInsets.getInsets(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.ime())
  5319	            if (rootInsets.isVisible(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.ime()) || imeInsets.bottom > 0) return true
  5320	        }
  5321	
  5322	        val rootHeight = rootView?.height ?: 0
  5323	        if (rootHeight <= 0) return false
  5324	        getWindowVisibleDisplayFrame(tempWindowRect)
  5325	        val obscuredHeight = rootHeight - tempWindowRect.height()
  5326	        return obscuredHeight > rootHeight * 0.18f
  5327	    }
  5328	
  5329	    private fun mockInputDimTarget(): Float {
  5330	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null || isMockSignInClosing()) return 0f
  5331	        if (expandedMockDropdown != null) return 1f
  5332	        val awaitingField = mockInputDimAwaitingField ?: return 0f
  5333	        return if (focusedMockField == awaitingField) 1f else 0f
  5334	    }

  5337	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null || isMockSignInClosing()) return 0f

  5340	        }
  5341	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) return 0f
  5342	        return if (
  5343	            focusedMockField != null ||
  5344	            activeMockFields().any { mockSignInFieldText(it).isNotEmpty() }
  5345	        ) {
  5346	            1f
  5347	        } else {
  5348	            0f
  5349	        }
  5350	    }

  5355	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.SignIn ||
  5356	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount ||
  5357	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword
  5358	        ) return 0f
  5359	        return MOCK_FORM_FOCUS_SHIFT_Y * formFocusMotion.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  5360	    }

  5379	    private fun mockSignInFieldLabel(field: MockSignInField): String {
  5380	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword && field == MockSignInField.Email) {
  5381	            return forgotRecoveryTarget.inputLabel
  5382	        }
  5383	
  5384	        return when (field) {
  5385	            MockSignInField.Username -> MOCK_FORM_USERNAME_TEXT
  5386	            MockSignInField.Password -> MOCK_FORM_PASSWORD_TEXT
  5387	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> MOCK_FORM_CONFIRM_PASSWORD_TEXT
  5388	            MockSignInField.Email -> MOCK_FORM_EMAIL_TEXT
  5389	            MockSignInField.Month -> MOCK_FORM_MONTH_TEXT
  5390	            MockSignInField.Day -> MOCK_FORM_DAY_TEXT
  5391	            MockSignInField.Year -> MOCK_FORM_YEAR_TEXT

  5394	
  5395	    private fun mockSignInFieldText(field: MockSignInField): String {
  5396	        return when (field) {
  5397	            MockSignInField.Username -> usernameText
  5398	            MockSignInField.Password -> passwordText
  5399	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> confirmPasswordText
  5400	            MockSignInField.Email -> emailText
  5401	            MockSignInField.Month -> monthText
  5402	            MockSignInField.Day -> dayText
  5403	            MockSignInField.Year -> yearText
  5404	        }
  5405	    }
  5406	
  5407	    private fun mockSignInFieldDisplayText(field: MockSignInField): String {
  5408	        return when (field) {
  5409	            MockSignInField.Username -> usernameText
  5410	            MockSignInField.Password -> "*".repeat(passwordText.length)
  5411	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> "*".repeat(confirmPasswordText.length)
  5412	            MockSignInField.Email -> emailText
  5413	            MockSignInField.Month -> monthText
  5414	            MockSignInField.Day -> dayText
  5415	            MockSignInField.Year -> yearText
  5416	        }
  5417	    }
  5418	
  5419	    private fun setMockSignInFieldText(field: MockSignInField, text: String) {
  5420	        val limitedText = text.take(MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS)
  5421	        val usernameWasEmpty = usernameText.isEmpty()
  5422	        when (field) {
  5423	            MockSignInField.Username -> usernameText = limitedText
  5424	            MockSignInField.Password -> passwordText = limitedText
  5425	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> confirmPasswordText = limitedText
  5426	            MockSignInField.Email -> emailText = limitedText
  5427	            MockSignInField.Month -> monthText = limitedText
  5428	            MockSignInField.Day -> dayText = limitedText
  5429	            MockSignInField.Year -> yearText = limitedText
  5430	        }
  5431	        if (field == MockSignInField.Username && usernameWasEmpty != usernameText.isEmpty()) {
  5432	            restartMockCreateUsernameRevealAnimation()
  5433	        }
  5434	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword && field == MockSignInField.Email) {
  5435	            resetMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  5436	        }
  5437	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  5438	        if (mockInputDimAwaitingField == field) {
  5439	            mockInputDimAwaitingField = null
  5440	        }
  5441	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  5442	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  5443	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  5444	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  5445	    }
  5446	
  5447	    private fun appendMockSignInInput(rawInput: String): Boolean {
  5448	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  5449	        val input = sanitizeMockSignInInput(rawInput)
  5450	        if (input.isEmpty()) return true
  5451	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  5452	        val baseText = currentText.dropLast(activeComposingText.length.coerceAtMost(currentText.length))
  5453	        val availableChars = (MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS - baseText.length).coerceAtLeast(0)
  5454	        val insertedText = input.take(availableChars)
  5455	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, baseText + insertedText)
  5456	        activeComposingText = ""
  5457	        return true
  5458	    }
  5459	
  5460	    private fun replaceMockSignInComposingText(rawInput: String): Boolean {
  5461	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  5462	        val input = sanitizeMockSignInInput(rawInput)
  5463	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  5464	        val baseText = currentText.dropLast(activeComposingText.length.coerceAtMost(currentText.length))
  5465	        val availableChars = (MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS - baseText.length).coerceAtLeast(0)
  5466	        val composingText = input.take(availableChars)
  5467	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, baseText + composingText)
  5468	        activeComposingText = composingText
  5469	        return true
  5470	    }
  5471	
  5472	    private fun finishMockSignInComposingText(): Boolean {
  5473	        activeComposingText = ""
  5474	        return true
  5475	    }
  5476	
  5477	    private fun deleteMockSignInInput(beforeLength: Int): Boolean {
  5478	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  5479	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  5480	        if (currentText.isEmpty()) {
  5481	            activeComposingText = ""
  5482	            return true
  5483	        }
  5484	        val deleteCount = beforeLength.coerceAtLeast(1).coerceAtMost(currentText.length)
  5485	        activeComposingText = ""
  5486	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, currentText.dropLast(deleteCount))
  5487	        return true
  5488	    }
  5489	
  5490	    private fun handleMockSignInEditorAction(actionCode: Int): Boolean {
  5491	        activeComposingText = ""
  5492	        val fields = activeMockFields()
  5493	        val fieldIndex = fields.indexOf(focusedMockField)
  5494	        if (fieldIndex >= 0 && fieldIndex < fields.lastIndex && actionCode != EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE) {
  5495	            focusMockSignInField(fields[fieldIndex + 1])
  5496	        } else if (
  5497	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword &&
  5498	            focusedMockField == MockSignInField.Email &&
  5499	            mockForgotPasswordCanSubmit()
  5500	        ) {
  5501	            startMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  5502	        } else {
  5503	            val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  5504	            inputMethodManager?.hideSoftInputFromWindow(windowToken, 0)
  5505	            clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  5506	        }
  5507	        return true
  5508	    }
  5509	
  5510	    private fun handleMockSignInKeyEvent(keyCode: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  5511	        if (event.action != KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) return true
  5512	        return when (keyCode) {
  5513	            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DEL -> deleteMockSignInInput(1)
  5514	            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ENTER -> handleMockSignInEditorAction(EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE)
  5515	            else -> {
  5516	                val typedCharacter = event.unicodeChar.takeIf { it != 0 }?.toChar()?.toString()
  5517	                typedCharacter?.let(::appendMockSignInInput) ?: false
  5518	            }
  5519	        }
  5520	    }
  5521	
  5522	    private fun sanitizeMockSignInInput(input: String): String {
  5523	        return buildString {
  5524	            input.forEach { character ->
  5525	                if (character != '\n' && character != '\r' && !character.isISOControl()) {
  5526	                    append(character)
  5527	                }
  5528	            }
  5529	        }
  5530	    }
  5531	
  5532	    private fun trailingFittingText(text: String, maxWidth: Float, paint: Paint): String {
  5533	        if (paint.measureText(text) <= maxWidth) return text
  5534	
  5535	        var startIndex = 0
  5536	        while (startIndex < text.length && paint.measureText(text.substring(startIndex)) > maxWidth) {
  5537	            startIndex += 1
  5538	        }
  5539	        return text.substring(startIndex)
  5540	    }
  5541	
  5542	    private fun wrappedTextLines(text: String, maxWidth: Float, paint: Paint): List<String> {
  5543	        val lines = mutableListOf<String>()
  5544	        text.split(Regex("\\n\\s*\\n")).forEachIndexed { paragraphIndex, paragraph ->
  5545	            if (paragraphIndex > 0) {
  5546	                lines += ""
  5547	            }
  5548	
  5549	            var currentLine = ""
  5550	            paragraph.replace('\n', ' ')
  5551	                .split(Regex("\\s+"))
  5552	                .filter { it.isNotEmpty() }
  5553	                .forEach { word ->
  5554	                    val candidateLine = if (currentLine.isEmpty()) word else "$currentLine $word"
  5555	                    if (currentLine.isEmpty() || paint.measureText(candidateLine) <= maxWidth) {
  5556	                        currentLine = candidateLine
  5557	                    } else {
  5558	                        lines += currentLine
  5559	                        currentLine = word
  5560	                    }
  5561	                }
  5562	            if (currentLine.isNotEmpty()) {
  5563	                lines += currentLine
  5564	            }
  5565	        }
  5566	        return lines
  5567	    }
  5568	
  5569	    private fun moveToward(current: Float, target: Float, amount: Float): Float {
  5570	        return when {
  5571	            current < target -> (current + amount).coerceAtMost(target)
  5572	            current > target -> (current - amount).coerceAtLeast(target)
  5573	            else -> current
  5574	        }
  5575	    }
  5576	
  5577	    private fun currentStageMetrics(): StageMetrics? {
  5578	        if (width <= 0 || height <= 0) return null
  5579	
  5580	        val standardScale = min(width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, height / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
  5581	        val portraitScale = portraitDesignScale()
  5582	        val portraitAmount = portraitIntroAmount()
  5583	        val stageScale = lerpFloat(standardScale, portraitScale, portraitAmount)
  5584	        val standardTop = (height - FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT * standardScale) / 2f
  5585	        return StageMetrics(
  5586	            left = (width - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stageScale) / 2f,
  5587	            top = lerpFloat(standardTop, 0f, portraitAmount) +
  5588	                mockAuthOpenStageLiftY(stageScale) +
  5589	                mockLandscapeInputStageLiftY(stageScale),
  5590	            scale = stageScale
  5591	        )
  5592	    }
  5593	
  5594	    private fun isPortraitIntroLayout(): Boolean = width <= height
  5595	
  5596	    private fun portraitIntroAmount(): Float {
  5597	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) return 0f
  5598	        // Keep the portrait welcome composition fixed while an auth panel slides in.
  5599	        return 1f
  5600	    }
  5601	
  5602	    private fun portraitDesignScale(): Float {
  5603	        if (width <= 0 || height <= 0) return 1f
  5604	
  5605	        val widthScale = width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH
  5606	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) return widthScale
  5607	
  5608	        val referenceDesignHeight = FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * PORTRAIT_REFERENCE_HEIGHT_TO_WIDTH
  5609	        return min(widthScale, height / referenceDesignHeight)
  5610	    }
  5611	
  5612	    private fun portraitSafeTop(): Float = systemBarInsetTopPx / portraitDesignScale()
  5613	
  5614	    private fun portraitSafeBottom(): Float {
  5615	        return (height - systemBarInsetBottomPx) / portraitDesignScale()
  5616	    }

  5618	    private fun portraitStackCenter(fraction: Float): Float {
  5619	        val top = portraitSafeTop()
  5620	        return top + (portraitSafeBottom() - top) * fraction
  5621	    }

  5714	    private fun portraitContentOffsetY(): Float {
  5715	        val logoHeight = LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE
  5716	        val rawLogoTop = portraitStackCenter(1f / 6f) - logoHeight / 2f
  5717	        val rawForgotCenter = portraitStackCenter(5f / 6f) + portraitPromptCenterSpacing()
  5718	        val topBreathingRoom = rawLogoTop + PORTRAIT_LOGO_WHITE_EYE_TOP_INSET - portraitSafeTop()
  5719	        val bottomBreathingRoom = portraitSafeBottom() -
  5720	            (rawForgotCenter + PORTRAIT_PROMPT_VISIBLE_HALF_HEIGHT)

  5727	        val rawForgotCenter = portraitStackCenter(5f / 6f) + portraitPromptCenterSpacing()
  5728	        val rawTopRoom = rawLogoTop + PORTRAIT_LOGO_WHITE_EYE_TOP_INSET - portraitSafeTop()
  5729	        val rawBottomRoom = portraitSafeBottom() -
  5730	            (rawForgotCenter + PORTRAIT_PROMPT_VISIBLE_HALF_HEIGHT)
  5731	        return (rawTopRoom + rawBottomRoom) / 2f
  5732	    }

  5737	            (PORTRAIT_COMFORTABLE_DESIGN_HEIGHT - PORTRAIT_COMPACT_DESIGN_HEIGHT))
  5738	            .coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  5739	    }

  5803	    private fun notifyIntroSnapshotChanged() {

  5808	        onIntroSnapshotChanged?.invoke(snapshot)
  5809	    }

  5811	    private fun switchMockAccountFlow(flow: MockAccountFlow) {
  5812	        if (
  5813	            mockSignInStartMillis == null ||
  5814	            activeMockFlow == flow ||
  5815	            isMockSignInClosing() ||
  5816	            isMockFlowSwitchAnimating()
  5817	        ) {
  5818	            return
  5819	        }
  5820	
  5821	        val previousFlow = activeMockFlow ?: return
  5822	        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  5823	        activeComposingText = ""
  5824	        expandedMockDropdown = null
  5825	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  5826	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  5827	        portraitAuthScrollY = 0f
  5828	        portraitAuthScrollMoved = false
  5829	        pressedMockField = null
  5830	        pressedRememberMe = false
  5831	        pressedBackChevron = false
  5832	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  5833	        activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
  5834	        mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
  5835	        mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
  5836	        lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
  5837	        pressedMockDropdown = null
  5838	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  5839	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  5840	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  5841	        resetMockCreateAvatarCarousel()
  5842	        if (flow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) {
  5843	            forgotRecoveryTarget = MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Password
  5844	            emailText = ""
  5845	            resetMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  5846	        }
  5847	        outgoingMockFlow = previousFlow
  5848	        activeMockFlow = flow
  5849	        mockFlowSwitchStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  5850	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  5851	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  5852	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  5853	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  5854	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  5855	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  5856	    }
